#248578 · SUBBTBIOCH_Q713940 DB2_PYQ UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_BT_BIOCHEM_MICRO — Biochemistry & General Microbiology (GATE BT) Medium PYQ: GATE BT (2021)Body: IIT-IISc · India
Domain: 1. Engineering & Technology · Unit: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Glycolysis, Gluconeogenesis, Pentose Phosphate Pathway · Topic: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Citric Acid (TCA) Cycle, Glyoxylate Cycle, Oxidative Phosphorylation & Ele

Which enzyme of the citric acid cycle is also a component of the mitochondrial electron transport chain?

A
Succinate dehydrogenase
✓ Correct
B
Aconitase
C
Fumarase
D
Malate dehydrogenase
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

Succinate dehydrogenase catalyzes oxidation of succinate to fumarate in the TCA cycle and also functions as Complex II, transferring electrons from FADH2 to ubiquinone. Aconitase, fumarase, and malate dehydrogenase are TCA cycle enzymes but are not components of the electron transport chain. Therefore, option A is correct.

#248577 · SUBBTBIOCH_Q713939 DB1_SCRAPED_MODIFIED 🖼️ Figure / Diagram UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_BT_BIOCHEM_MICRO — Biochemistry & General Microbiology (GATE BT) Easy PYQ: N/A (N/A)Body: N/A · N/A
Domain: 1. Engineering & Technology · Unit: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Glycolysis, Gluconeogenesis, Pentose Phosphate Pathway · Topic: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Citric Acid (TCA) Cycle, Glyoxylate Cycle, Oxidative Phosphorylation & Ele

Which complex of the mitochondrial electron transport chain does not pump protons from the matrix to the intermembrane space?

A
Complex I
B
Complex II
✓ Correct
C
Complex III
D
Complex IV
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

Complex I, Complex III, and Complex IV are proton pumps that contribute to the proton-motive force. Complex II, succinate dehydrogenase, accepts electrons from FADH2 and passes them to ubiquinone, but its free-energy change is insufficient for proton translocation. Therefore, Complex II is the correct answer.

#248576 · SUBBTBIOCH_Q713938 DB1_SCRAPED_MODIFIED UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_BT_BIOCHEM_MICRO — Biochemistry & General Microbiology (GATE BT) Medium PYQ: N/A (N/A)Body: N/A · N/A
Domain: 1. Engineering & Technology · Unit: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Glycolysis, Gluconeogenesis, Pentose Phosphate Pathway · Topic: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Citric Acid (TCA) Cycle, Glyoxylate Cycle, Oxidative Phosphorylation & Ele

Why does the glyoxylate cycle permit net synthesis of carbohydrate from acetyl-CoA, unlike the standard citric acid cycle?

A
It bypasses both decarboxylation reactions that release CO2 in the TCA cycle.
✓ Correct
B
It converts every acetyl-CoA directly into glucose without intermediates.
C
It generates more NADH than the TCA cycle per acetyl-CoA.
D
It replaces succinate dehydrogenase with a proton-pumping complex.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

The glyoxylate cycle uses isocitrate lyase and malate synthase to convert two acetyl-CoA molecules into succinate while avoiding the CO2-releasing steps catalyzed by isocitrate dehydrogenase and alpha-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase. This preserves carbon for gluconeogenesis. It does not convert acetyl-CoA directly to glucose, does not primarily increase NADH yield, and does not replace succinate dehydrogenase with a proton pump. Thus, A is correct.

#248575 · SUBBTBIOCH_Q713937 DB1_SCRAPED_MODIFIED 🖼️ Figure / Diagram UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_BT_BIOCHEM_MICRO — Biochemistry & General Microbiology (GATE BT) Medium PYQ: N/A (N/A)Body: N/A · N/A
Domain: 1. Engineering & Technology · Unit: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Glycolysis, Gluconeogenesis, Pentose Phosphate Pathway · Topic: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Citric Acid (TCA) Cycle, Glyoxylate Cycle, Oxidative Phosphorylation & Ele

In the citric acid cycle, which enzyme catalyzes the oxidative decarboxylation of isocitrate to alpha-ketoglutarate?

A
Isocitrate dehydrogenase
✓ Correct
B
Alpha-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase
C
Succinate dehydrogenase
D
Malate dehydrogenase
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

Isocitrate dehydrogenase oxidizes isocitrate and releases CO2, forming alpha-ketoglutarate and NADH. Alpha-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase acts on the next intermediate, not isocitrate. Succinate dehydrogenase oxidizes succinate to fumarate and does not decarboxylate. Malate dehydrogenase regenerates oxaloacetate from malate. Therefore, option A is correct.

#248574 · SUBGSGK_Q711596 DB2_PYQ UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_GS_GK — Multidisciplinary Competitive Exam & General Studies Medium PYQ: Global & National Competitive Exam (ceval-exam) (2021-2024)Body: Official Academic & Professional Exam Benchmark · ALL_COUNTRIES
Domain: Competitive Exams · Unit: Probability And Statistics · Topic: Probability And Statistics (ZH Exam Paper)

设二维随机变量(X,Y)的概率密度函数为:f(x,y)={a(x+y),0<x<2,0<y<10,其他,则常数a=____

A
13
✓ Correct
B
2
C
3
D
12
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

1. 首先，对于二维随机变量(X,Y)，其概率密度函数f(x,y)需满足归一性，即∬−∞∞f(x,y)dxdy=1。 2. 其次，我们需要计算概率密度函数在定义域内的二重积分。根据题目给定的定义域，我们有：∬02∬01a(x+y)dydx。 3. 接着，我们计算二重积分。首先对y积分，得到∫01a(x+y)dy=a[x+12(y2)]01=a(x+12)。然后对x积分，得到∫02a(x+12)dx=a[12(x2+x)]02=3a。 4. 最后，根据归一性，我们有3a=1，解得常数a=13。

#248573 · SUBGSGK_Q711594 DB2_PYQ UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_GS_GK — Multidisciplinary Competitive Exam & General Studies Medium PYQ: Global & National Competitive Exam (ceval-exam) (2021-2024)Body: Official Academic & Professional Exam Benchmark · ALL_COUNTRIES
Domain: Competitive Exams · Unit: Probability And Statistics · Topic: Probability And Statistics (ZH Exam Paper)

独立地测量一个物理量,记每次测量的结果为X=μ+ε,其中μ是物理量的真值,ε是测量产生的随机误差,且已知每次测量产生的随机误差都服从(−1,1)上的均匀分布,如果取n次测量结果的算术平均值X¯=164∑i=164Xi作为真值μ的近似值,用切比雪夫不等式估计|X¯−μ|<18的概率的结果:____

A
不超过 23
B
不小于 13
C
不小于 23
✓ Correct
D
不超过 13
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

1. 首先，我们需要知道切比雪夫不等式：对于任意随机变量X，其期望为μ，方差为σ^2，那么对于任意正数k，有P(|X−μ|≥kσ)≤1k2。 2. 其次，我们需要计算随机误差ε的期望和方差。由题意知，ε服从(−1,1)上的均匀分布，其期望E(ε)=0，方差Var(ε)=2212=13。 3. 接着，我们计算X¯的期望和方差。由于每次测量都是独立的，所以E(X¯)=E(164∑i=164Xi)=μ，Var(X¯)=Var(164∑i=164Xi)=1642∑i=164Var(Xi)=164·13。 4. 最后，我们利用切比雪夫不等式估计P(|X¯−μ|<18)。由切比雪夫不等式，我们有P(|X¯−μ|≥18)≤Var(X¯)(18)2=164·13(18)2=13。所以，P(|X¯−μ|<18)≥1−13=23。 因此，|X¯−μ|<18的概率不小于23，答案是C。

#248572 · SUBGSGK_Q711592 DB2_PYQ UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_GS_GK — Multidisciplinary Competitive Exam & General Studies Medium PYQ: Global & National Competitive Exam (ceval-exam) (2021-2024)Body: Official Academic & Professional Exam Benchmark · ALL_COUNTRIES
Domain: Competitive Exams · Unit: Probability And Statistics · Topic: Probability And Statistics (ZH Exam Paper)

设随机变量(X,Y)的联合密度函数是:f(x,y)={ke−x−2y,x>0,y>00,其他则P(0≤X≤2,0≤Y≤1)=____

A
(1−e−4)2
B
(1−e−8)2
C
(1−e−2)2
✓ Correct
D
(1−e−6)2
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

1. 首先，我们需要找到常数k。为了使f(x, y)成为一个有效的联合密度函数，我们需要满足∫−∞∞∫−∞∞f(x,y)dxdy=1。由于f(x, y)在x>0和y>0的区域内为非零值，我们可以将积分限改为∫0∞∫0∞ke−x−2ydxdy=1。 2. 计算积分，得到k∫0∞∫0∞e−x−2ydxdy=k∫0∞(−e−x−2y|x=0x=∞)dy=k∫0∞e−2ydy=k(−12e−2y|y=0y=∞)=k12=1。解得k=2。 3. 现在我们已经知道联合密度函数f(x,y)={2e−x−2y,x>0,y>00,其他。接下来，我们需要计算P(0≤X≤2,0≤Y≤1)，即在给定范围内的联合密度函数的积分：P(0≤X≤2,0≤Y≤1)=∫01∫022e−x−2ydxdy。 4. 计算积分，得到P(0≤X≤2,0≤Y≤1)=∫01(−e−x−2y|x=0x=2)dy=∫01(e−2y−e−2−2y)dy=(−12e−2y|y=0y=1)+12(e−2−2y|y=0y=1)=(1−e−2)2。

#248571 · SUBAPTQUAN_Q709808 DB2_PYQ UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_APT_QUANT — Quantitative Aptitude, Arithmetic, Data Interpretation & Elementary Mathematics Medium PYQ: Global & National Competitive Exam (ceval-exam) (2021-2024)Body: Official Academic & Professional Exam Benchmark · ALL_COUNTRIES
Domain: Aptitude · Unit: Middle School Mathematics · Topic: Middle School Mathematics (ZH Exam Paper)

已知关于x的不等式组{x<3a+2,2x−4>0,恰有两个整数解，则a的取值范围为____

A
2≤a≤3
B
23<a≤1
✓ Correct
C
2<a≤3
D
23≤a<1
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

1. 首先，我们需要解第二个不等式2x−4>0。将其化简得到x>2。 2. 接着，我们将两个不等式组合在一起，得到x>2且x<3a+2。由题意，这个不等式组恰有两个整数解，因此，x=3和x=4。 3. 最后，我们需要找到a的取值范围。将这两个x取值代入不等式x<3a+2，得到3<3a+2和4<3a+2。解这两个不等式，我们得到a>13和a>23，合并两个条件得到a>23。由于题目要求恰有两个整数解，因此，x<5。所以有x<3a+2≤5，化简后得到a≤1。因此，a的取值范围为23<a≤1。

#248570 · SUBSCICHEM_Q709237 DB2_PYQ UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_SCI_CHEMISTRY — Chemistry (Physical, Inorganic, Organic & Analytical) Medium PYQ: Global & National Competitive Exam (ceval-exam) (2021-2024)Body: Official Academic & Professional Exam Benchmark · ALL_COUNTRIES
Domain: Science · Unit: Middle School Chemistry · Topic: Middle School Chemistry (ZH Exam Paper)

取一定量的氧化铁与氧化铜的混合物，加入溶质质量分数为9.8%的稀硫酸100g，恰好完全反应生成盐和水.原混合物中氧元素的质量是____

A
6.4g
B
3.2g
C
1.8g
D
1.6g
✓ Correct
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option D)

1. 首先，我们需要知道氧化铁和氧化铜与稀硫酸反应的化学方程式：Fe2O3+3H2SO4→Fe2(SO4)3+3H2O；CuO+H2SO4→CuSO4+H2O。根据题目描述，这两个反应都发生了，且完全反应。 2. 其次，分析化学方程式可知氧化物中氧元素全部转变为水中的氧元素。 3. 接着，我们需要计算生成的水的质量。设生成水的质量是x g：由方程式可以看出硫酸与生成水的关系为98100×8%=18x,解得x=1.8 g,所以水中氧元素的质量为：1.8g×1618=1.6g。

#248569 · SUBAPTQUAN_Q706814 DB2_PYQ UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_APT_QUANT — Quantitative Aptitude, Arithmetic, Data Interpretation & Elementary Mathematics Medium PYQ: Global & National Competitive Exam (ceval-exam) (2021-2024)Body: Official Academic & Professional Exam Benchmark · ALL_COUNTRIES
Domain: Aptitude · Unit: High School Mathematics · Topic: High School Mathematics (ZH Exam Paper)

在矩形ABCD种，AB=1,AD=2,则点P在以点C为圆心且与BD相切的圆上，若AP→=λAB→+μAD→,则λ+μ的最大值为____

A
3
✓ Correct
B
22
C
5
D
2
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

1. 首先，我们需要求圆的半径。BD长为12+22=5，圆的半径为C到BD做垂线的长度，即1×25=255。 2. 设A点为原点，AD为x轴，AB为y轴，则点P坐标需要满足的圆的参数方程可写为x=2+255cosθ,y=1+255sinθ，又有λ和μ满足x=2μ，y=λ，与圆的参数方程联立可得μ=1+55cosθ,λ=1+255sinθ。 3. 整理可得λ+μ=2+255sinθ+55cosθ，设ϕ满足sinϕ=55,cosϕ=255，则有λ+μ=2+sin(θ+ϕ)≤3。

一个C语言程序在一台32位机器上运行，程序中定义了3个变量x、y、z，其中x和z是int型，y为short型。当x=127，y=-9时，执行赋值语句z=x+y后，x、y、z的值分别是____。

A
x=0000007FH，y=FFF9H，z=00000076H
B
x=0000007FH，y=FFF9H，z=FFFF0076H
C
x=0000007FH，y=FFF7H，z=FFFF0076H
D
x=0000007FH，y=FFF7H，z=00000076H
✓ Correct
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option D)

1. x和z为int型，说明x和z都占32位的存储空间。 2. 127换成二进制为0000 0000 0000 0000 0000 0000 0111 1111，对应的十六进制为0000007FH。z进行运算后变成118，换成二进制为0000 0000 0000 0000 0000 0000 0111 0110，对应的十六进制为00000076H。 3. 另外，因为y为short型，所以y所占存储空间为16位，且在计算机中使用补码表示（默认的）。-9的二进制表示为1000 0000 0000 1001，因此-9的补码表示为1111 1111 1111 0111(符号位不变，其余位取反加1)，对应的十六进制为FFF7H。

光子能量为0.5MeV的X射线，入射到某种物质上而发生康普顿散射．若反冲电子的能量为0.1MeV，则散射光波长的改变量Δλ与入射光波长λ_0之比值为____

A
0.2
B
0.25
✓ Correct
C
0.3
D
0.35
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

1. 首先，我们需要将入射光子能量和散射光子能量转换成波长。可以使用公式E=h*c/λ，其中E表示能量，λ表示波长。入射光子能量为0.5 MeV，对应波长λ0=h*c/(0.5MeV)。反冲电子能量为0.1 MeV，因此散射光子能量为0.4 MeV，对应波长λ′=h*c/(0.4MeV)。 2. 其次，我们计算Δλ=λ′−λ0=h*c/(0.4MeV)−h*c/(0.5MeV)。 3. 最后，我们要求(Δλ/λ0)=[(h*c/(0.4MeV)−h*c/(0.5MeV))/(h*c/(0.5MeV))]。化简得到比值为0.25。

A
m2g2/(4k)
B
m2g2/(3k)
C
m2g2/(2k)
✓ Correct
D
2m2g2/k
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

1. 当小球刚好脱离地面时，弹簧的弹力与小球所受重力相等，即kx=mg，其中x表示弹簧的伸长量。 2. 由上述方程可得，x=mg/k。 3. 外力作功等于弹簧势能的增加，即W=12kx2−0（因为开始时弹簧为原长，势能为0）。 4. 将第2步中得到的x代入第3步的公式，得到W=12k(mg/k)2=m2g2/(2k)。

#248565 · SUBGSINDIA_Q702759 DB2_PYQ COUNTRY_LOCAL: India SUB_GS_INDIAN_ECONOMY — Indian Economy, Economic Development, Planning, Budgeting & Social Sector Medium PYQ: Global & National Competitive Exam (ceval-exam) (2021-2024)Body: Official Academic & Professional Exam Benchmark · ALL_COUNTRIES

已知一垄断企业成本函数为TC=5Q2+20Q+10，产品的需求函数为Q=140-P。利润最大化的产量为____。

A
10
✓ Correct
B
5
C
3
D
15
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

1. 首先，垄断企业的利润最大化产量是当边际收益MR等于边际成本MC时。因此，我们需要求出该企业的边际收益和边际成本函数。 2. 其次，求出总收益TR。TR=P*Q=140Q−Q2。 3. 接着，通过总收益TR求出边际收益MR函数，即MR=ΔTRΔQ=140−2Q。 4. 最后，通过成本函数TC求出边际成本MC函数，即MC=ΔTCΔQ=20+10Q。 5. 根据上述计算，我们将边际收益函数和边际成本函数相等，即140−2Q=20+10Q，解得Q=10。因此，利润最大化的产量为10。

考虑以下小型开放经济的数据：Y=1000，C=700，G=150，I=250-1000r*。如果世界利率为5%，那么小型开放经济的净出口为____。

A
50
B
-50
✓ Correct
C
150
D
-150
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

1. 首先，小型开放经济的净出口公式：NX = S - I，其中NX表示净出口，S表示储蓄，I表示投资。 2. 其次，根据国民账户恒等式，Y = C + I + G + NX，可以得到S = Y - C - G。将数据代入得到S = 150。 3. 接着，根据题目中的数据计算投资，I = 250 - 1000r*，其中r*表示世界利率。将r* = 5%代入得到I = 200。 4. 最后，计算净出口。将步骤2和步骤3的结果代入净出口公式，得到NX = S - I = 150 - 200 = -50。

下列物质加入电池负极溶液中，使Zn2+/Zn-H+/H2组成的原电池电动势增大的是____

A
ZnSO4固体
B
Zn粒
C
Na2S溶液
✓ Correct
D
Na2SO4固体
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

1. 首先，我们需要了解Zn2+/Zn-H+/H2组成的原电池，其中Zn2+/Zn是负极，H+/H2是正极。 2. 其次，增大正极的电极电势或降低负极的电极电势可使电池电动势增大。显然，降低负极氧化型的浓度能使负极的电极电势减小。 3. 分析选项： (A)ZnSO4固体，(B) Zn粒和(D)Na2SO4固体，加入后不会改变电池的还原反应和氧化反应，因此不会使电池电动势增大；(C) Na2S溶液，加入S2−使大部分Zn2+被沉淀下来，降低了Zn2+的浓度，负极电势减小。 4. 根据以上分析，加入Na2S溶液可以使Zn2+/Zn-H+/H2组成的原电池电动势增大。

求定积分：∫01(1−x)2x−x2dx=____

A
12
B
13
✓ Correct
C
14
D
15
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

1. 首先，我们观察到积分中的2x−x2，可以首先考虑进行变换。有(1−x)2x−x2=(1−x)1−(1−x)2。 2. 其次，考虑使用换元积分法进行求解。令1−x=u⇒x=1−u⇒dx=d(1−u)=−du。 3. 接着，我们需要将积分区间进行变换。当x=0时，u=1；当x=1时，u=0，因此积分区间变为[1,0]。 4. 将换元后的x代入原式，得到：∫01(1−x)2x−x2dx=∫10u1−u2(−du)=∫01u1−u2du。 5. 再次使用换元积分法，令u=sint⇒du=d(sint)=costdt，当u=0时，t=π2；当u=1时，t=0，因此，积分区间变为[0,π2]。 6. 最后，将换元后的u代入第4步的表达式，得到∫01(1−x)2x−x2=∫01u1−u2du=∫0π2sint1−sin2t·costdt=∫0π2sintcos2tdt=13。

Àwọn àṣàyàn ìlànà ààbò abẹ́lé wo ló wà fún àwọn ọlọ́lá?

A
Àwọn olórí ẹ̀dá máa ń dá àwọn ohun tí kò dára sílẹ̀ fún àìsí ìtìlẹ́yìn fún ìjọba. Àtúnpínpín gẹ́gẹ́ bí ọ̀nà ìkọ̀sílẹ̀ fún ẹ̀ka agbára ní ọ̀nà ti "ìkọ̀sílẹ̀ ẹ̀tọ́ ọmọnìyàn fún àwọn ògbóǹkangí" èyí tí ó lè ní àyípadà àwọn ohun àmúṣọrọ̀, ìdádúró àti ìkọ̀sílẹ̀ àyè sí àwọn ọ̀nà ìgbòkègbodò ìṣòwò.
B
Àìní agbára àwọn ohun èlò ìkọ́lé àti ẹ̀tọ́ àwùjọ tó gbòòrò, àwọn ọlọ́lá ìpínlẹ̀ tí kò lágbára ni wọ́n sábà máa ń fipá mú láti gbára lé agbára agbára. Síbẹ̀síbẹ̀, ìṣòro pàtàkì kan ni pé àwọn ohun èlò ìwà ipá lè jẹ́ ewu fún ìjọba tí ó nílò ìpalára mọ̀ọ́mọ̀ọ́mọ̀ fún àwọn ọmọ ogun.
✓ Correct
C
Àwọn ọlọ́lá gbìyànjú láti ṣe ìgbéga ìṣèlú ìdánimọ̀ tuntun nínú èyí tí ìmọ̀ ẹ̀yà rọ́pò nípasẹ̀ àwọn èrò tí ó ní ìtọ́sọ́nà nípa ìtàn àròsọ ìjọba.
D
Àwọn ìpínlẹ̀ tí kò lágbára ti ṣe àtìlẹ́yìn àgbáyé tí wọ́n sì ti bẹ̀rẹ̀ àwọn ìlànà àtúnṣe ìjọba tiwa-n-tiwa kí wọ́n lè gba ẹ̀tọ́ gbogbo ènìyàn nínú nígbà tí wọ́n ń ṣe ìgbélárugẹ ẹ̀tọ́ àgbáyé rẹ̀ ní ìta.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

Correct Answer: (B) Àìní agbára àwọn ohun èlò ìkọ́lé àti ẹ̀tọ́ àwùjọ tó gbòòrò, àwọn ọlọ́lá ìpínlẹ̀ tí kò lágbára ni wọ́n sábà máa ń fipá mú láti gbára lé agbára agbára. Síbẹ̀síbẹ̀, ìṣòro pàtàkì kan ni pé àwọn ohun èlò ìwà ipá lè jẹ́ ewu fún ìjọba tí ó nílò ìpalára mọ̀ọ́mọ̀ọ́mọ̀ fún àwọn ọmọ ogun. [Subject: Security Studies · Medium: EN].

Ní àwọn ọ̀nà wo, tí ó bá wà, ṣé àyíká lè jẹ́ àníyàn ààbò?

A
Ààbò àyíká ń mú kí àgbéyẹ̀wò ààbò àyíká àgbáyé, pẹ̀lú àwọn ètò-ìpínlẹ̀ tí ó wà nínú rẹ̀ àti àwọn ètò àjọ tí ó wà ní ẹ̀yìn ààlà orílẹ̀-èdè.
B
Aabo ayika jẹ iṣẹ akanṣe aabo to ṣe pataki ni pe o beere tani ati kini lati ni aabo ati lati iru irokeke wo nipasẹ awọn eto imulo aabo, tabi boya awọn asopọ laarin ayika, aabo ati awọn ọran idagbasoke le ṣee ṣe.
C
Ààbò àyíká jẹ́ ìgbésẹ̀ tó wúlò láti ṣàyẹ̀wò bí àyíká ṣe ń yí padà tó sì ń fa rògbòdìyàn láàárín àwọn orílẹ̀-èdè àti láàárín àwọn orílẹ̀-èdè, àti àwọn ọ̀nà tí ààbò àyíká lè gbà ba ààbò orílẹ̀-èdè jẹ́.
D
Gbogbo awọn aṣayan wọnyi. Àyíká náà jẹ́ nkan tí a gbọ́dọ̀ dáàbò bo àti orísun ewu, bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ pé ó lè túmọ̀ sí oríṣiríṣi nkan sí oríṣiríṣi ènìyàn. Bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ pé ó ń mú kí èrò ààbò jinlẹ̀ ó ní ìwọ̀n tó ṣe pàtàkì tí ó sì wúlò bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ pé ìwúlò èrò náà jẹ́ ìfigagbága.
✓ Correct
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option D)

Correct Answer: (D) Gbogbo awọn aṣayan wọnyi. Àyíká náà jẹ́ nkan tí a gbọ́dọ̀ dáàbò bo àti orísun ewu, bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ pé ó lè túmọ̀ sí oríṣiríṣi nkan sí oríṣiríṣi ènìyàn. Bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ pé ó ń mú kí èrò ààbò jinlẹ̀ ó ní ìwọ̀n tó ṣe pàtàkì tí ó sì wúlò bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ pé ìwúlò èrò náà jẹ́ ìfigagbága. [Subject: Security Studies · Medium: EN].

Látìgbà tí Ogun Tutu ti parí, báwo ni ọ̀nà tí àwọn orílẹ̀-èdè tó ń ṣàkóso nílẹ̀ Yúróòpù ń gbà lo ọgbọ́n ìṣèlú tó ń múni ṣe ohun tí kò tọ́ ṣe ti yí padà?

A
Iṣẹ́-ìfipá-nipasẹ̀-ẹ̀rí ti fi hàn pé ó jẹ́ ọ̀nà ìṣèlú tó ṣàṣeyọrí àti èyí tí ó jẹ́ apá pàtàkì nínú ètò ìdarí ìjà. Àwọn àṣeyọrí tí àwọn ìgbésẹ̀ ètò ìṣèlú tó ti kọjá ní àwùjọ lẹ́yìn 9/11 ti mú ìyípadà sí ọ̀nà ìfọ̀rọ̀wánilẹ́nuwò lílágbára lágbára nípa mímú ìfẹ́ ọkàn ìṣèlú Amẹ́ríkà pọ̀ sí i láti kojú ìpániláyà tí ó ń mú ọ̀pọ̀lọpọ̀ ènìyàn ṣòfò ní àwọn orílẹ̀-èdè tí ó ń kùnà.
B
Ayika agbegbe-iṣelu ti o wuyi, ti o jẹ afihan nipasẹ agbara giga ologun ti Iwọ-oorun ti o lagbara pupọ ati eewu kekere ti idasi agbara nla ọta, ti ṣe ipilẹṣẹ awọn igara ati awọn iwuri fun awọn oluṣeto imulo Iwọ-oorun lati ṣe agbega ero eto imulo wọn, ati lati ṣakoso awọn ija ologun lati awọn ipinlẹ alailagbara ati awọn ipinlẹ ti kuna.
✓ Correct
C
Ìparun ìyapa èrò orí tí ó pín àwùjọ àgbáyé sí méjì nígbà Ogun Abẹ́lé, dípò kí ó yọrí sí dídín ìjà kù, ó fi ogún tí kò dúró sán-ún sílẹ̀ níbi tí òṣèlú nípa lílo agbára àti ìgbẹ̀san tí ó pọ̀jù ti ń bá a lọ láti jẹ́ ewu gidi. Ewu ti ilosoke ti ko ni iṣakoso ṣi wa ṣugbọn ni ọna ti o yatọ; Ọ̀pọ̀lọpọ̀ ìwà ìbàjẹ́ ọ̀pọ̀lọpọ̀, àkóràn láti àwọn ìpínlẹ̀ tí ó kùnà àti àwọn òṣèré tí kì í ṣe ti ìpínlẹ̀ tí wọ́n ń wá ọ̀nà láti gba WMD ni àníyàn àkọ́kọ́ fún àwọn ìpínlẹ̀ tí wọ́n sì nílò ìdáhùn ní ìrísí ìgbésẹ̀ àwọn ológun.
D
Àyíká ọ̀tá tó wà láyìíká àwọn orílẹ̀-èdè ti mú kí àwọn orílẹ̀-èdè máa lo ọgbọ́n ológun kí wọ́n lè dáàbò bo àwọn ohun tó jẹ àwọn lógún. Lílo ìwà ipá dípò ìfọ̀rọ̀wéròwérò tàbí ìwúrí ti di ìdáhùn àkọ́kọ́ sí àwọn ewu, yálà àwọn wọ̀nyí jẹ́ èyí tí àwọn orílẹ̀-èdè mìíràn gbé kalẹ̀, àìtó àwọn ohun àmúṣọrọ̀, àwọn àrùn tó ń ranni, tàbí àwọn ewu mìíràn tí kì í ṣe ti ìbílẹ̀.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

Correct Answer: (B) Ayika agbegbe-iṣelu ti o wuyi, ti o jẹ afihan nipasẹ agbara giga ologun ti Iwọ-oorun ti o lagbara pupọ ati eewu kekere ti idasi agbara nla ọta, ti ṣe ipilẹṣẹ awọn igara ati awọn iwuri fun awọn oluṣeto imulo Iwọ-oorun lati ṣe agbega ero eto imulo wọn, ati lati ṣakoso awọn ija ologun lati awọn ipinlẹ alailagbara ati awọn ipinlẹ ti kuna. Kini awọn okunfa ti ipanilaya?

A
Ìjọba ìjọba orílẹ̀-èdè àti ìfẹ́ orílẹ̀-èdè ń dènà àfààní fún ìpànìyàn: ìfẹ́ orílẹ̀-èdè ń pèsè agbára èrò láti ṣe ìṣọ̀kan ìyàtọ̀ ẹ̀yà, àti pé ìjọba tiwa-n-tiwa ń gba ààyè fún àlàyé àwọn ìkórìíra ní ọ̀nà tí ó dára. Láàárín àtúpalẹ̀ yìí àwọn ìpínlẹ̀ tí wọ́n ní ìwé-ẹ̀rí ìjọba ìjọba àti olùfẹ́ orílẹ̀-èdè tí kò lágbára lè pèsè àwọn ipò tí ó ń ṣe ìrànlọ́wọ́ fún ìdàgbàsókè ìpànìyàn.
B
Ìwà ìbàjẹ́ láàárín àwọn ẹgbẹ́ Mùsùlùmí ni wọ́n ṣe àgbékalẹ̀ rẹ̀ lórí àwọn ìlà ẹ̀yà tí ó tako ẹ̀sìn tí ó tẹ̀lé ìgbàlódé. Ó lè jẹ́ ohun ìyàlẹ́nu pé bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ pé wọ́n ṣètò ìpànìyàn ní àyíká ìdẹ́rùbà sí àwùjọ Mùsùlùmí ní Àárín Ìlà Oòrùn, ìdáhùn sí ìdẹ́rùbà ti yọrí sí àwọn ẹgbẹ́ ní Europe tí wọ́n ní ìdẹ́rùbà nípasẹ̀ ìfọwọ́sowọ́pọ̀ àwọn Mùsùlùmí lórí àwọn ìwà Ìwọ̀ Oòrùn.
C
Àwọn iṣẹ́ ọjà olówó-nkan àti ìyípadà ìmọ̀-ẹ̀rọ àti ìbánisọ̀rọ̀ ti pọ̀ sí i lórí àṣà àti àwùjọ ìbílẹ̀. Àìdọ́gba ìdàgbàsókè àti ìyàsọ́tọ̀ àwọn ẹ̀yà tàbí ètò ọrọ̀ ajé kan nínú àwọn ènìyàn, pẹ̀lú ìmọ̀ tí ó ń pọ̀ sí i nípa ìyàsọ́tọ̀ wọn ń pèsè ìdí fún ìbísí ìwà ipá.
✓ Correct
D
Awọn eto ijọba ti kii ṣe tiwantiwa ti o ṣe afihan nipasẹ eto aarin ti o lagbara ati awọn ọna ipaniyan ṣii aaye fun ibanujẹ ti o gbajumọ lati yipada si awọn iṣẹ apanilaya ti iṣiṣẹ pẹlu ero lati pari eto iṣelu. Bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ pé àwọn àfààní fún ìkópa àwọn ènìyàn nínú àwọn ìjọba olómìnira ń dènà àìtẹ́lọ́rùn àwọn ènìyàn láti ṣàlàyé rẹ̀ ní ọ̀nà tí kò dára.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

Correct Answer: (C) Àwọn iṣẹ́ ọjà olówó-nkan àti ìyípadà ìmọ̀-ẹ̀rọ àti ìbánisọ̀rọ̀ ti pọ̀ sí i lórí àṣà àti àwùjọ ìbílẹ̀. Àìdọ́gba ìdàgbàsókè àti ìyàsọ́tọ̀ àwọn ẹ̀yà tàbí ètò ọrọ̀ ajé kan nínú àwọn ènìyàn, pẹ̀lú ìmọ̀ tí ó ń pọ̀ sí i nípa ìyàsọ́tọ̀ wọn ń pèsè ìdí fún ìbísí ìwà ipá. [Subject: Security Studies · Medium: EN].

Kí ni Àwọn Ìwádìí Ààbò Tó Ṣe Kókó?

A
Ọ̀nà sí ààbò tí ó kan ìmọ̀ tó ṣe kókó fún àwọn ẹ̀sìn olódodo tí wọ́n ti fìdí múlẹ̀ fún ìwádìí ààbò.
✓ Correct
B
Ìlànà ààbò pàtó tí ó ń ṣe ìdánimọ̀ àwọn aṣojú tí a ṣàlàyé kedere, ìdẹ́rùbà, àti ìlànà.
C
Ọ̀rọ̀ kejì fún Copenhagen School of Security.
D
Ààbò ìwádìí nínú èyí tí ó ṣe pàtàkì fún ìpínlẹ̀, àwọn ológun àti ààbò ìbílẹ̀ wà ní ìtọ́jú.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

Correct Answer: (A) Ọ̀nà sí ààbò tí ó kan ìmọ̀ tó ṣe kókó fún àwọn ẹ̀sìn olódodo tí wọ́n ti fìdí múlẹ̀ fún ìwádìí ààbò. [Subject: Security Studies · Medium: EN].

Ọ̀rọ̀ wo ló ṣàpèjúwe ìwà Booth sí ìkọ́lé àti lẹ́yìn ìṣètò láàárín Àwọn Ìwádìí Ààbò Tó Ṣe Kókó?

A
Lẹ́yìn ìṣètò kò pèsè ìpìlẹ̀ kankan fún ìgbésẹ̀ òṣèlú, jíjẹ́ àpòpọ̀ ewu ti ìwà ìbàjẹ́ èké, ìbáṣepọ̀ àti ìkọ̀kọ̀, nígbà tí ìkọ́lé jẹ́ ìlànà sí òṣèlú àgbáyé dípò ìlànà òṣèlú pàtó.
✓ Correct
B
Constructivism ń pèsè ọ̀pọ̀lọpọ̀ àwọn ìlànà òṣèlú pàtó tí ó wúlò fún Àwọn Ìwádìí Ààbò Tó Ṣe Kókó ṣùgbọ́n gbogbo àwọn wọ̀nyí kò ní àìlágbára tí a nílò nínú ìwádìí ààbò. Àwọn ìlànà lẹ́yìn ìṣètò bákan náà kò ní agbára tí àwọn ọ̀mọ̀wé CSS fẹ́.
C
Lẹ́yìn ìṣètò àti ìkọ́lé jẹ́ àwọn ìlànà tí kò ní ìtumọ̀ tí wọ́n ṣàpèjúwe rẹ̀ dáadáa gẹ́gẹ́ bí ìlànà òṣèlú, tí ó wúlò ní àwọn ọ̀rọ̀ tí kò dára ṣùgbọ́n tí kò ní nkan tí àwọn ọ̀mọ̀wé nílò nínú ìwádìí ààbò ìgbàlódé.
D
Constructivism léwu jù àpòpọ̀ àwọn èrò òṣèlú, àpapọ̀ àwọn èròjà ìdàgbàsókè àti ìbáṣepọ̀. Lẹ́yìn ìṣètò jẹ́ ìlànà sí òṣèlú àgbáyé dípò ìlànà òṣèlú pàtó tí ó sì wúlò.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

Correct Answer: (A) Lẹ́yìn ìṣètò kò pèsè ìpìlẹ̀ kankan fún ìgbésẹ̀ òṣèlú, jíjẹ́ àpòpọ̀ ewu ti ìwà ìbàjẹ́ èké, ìbáṣepọ̀ àti ìkọ̀kọ̀, nígbà tí ìkọ́lé jẹ́ ìlànà sí òṣèlú àgbáyé dípò ìlànà òṣèlú pàtó. [Subject: Security Studies · Medium: EN].

Ó jẹ́ olùdarí ìbánisọ̀rọ̀ fún apa ibi kan ní ilé iṣẹ́ tí kìíṣe ti èyí tó wà fún èrè jíjẹ tí ó jẹ ara ilé iṣẹ́ ńlá ti orílẹ̀ èdè tí kìíṣe èyí tó wà fún èrè jíjẹ. Wọ́n máa ńsáàbà béèrè lọ́wọ́ rẹ pé kí o ṣètò ìtànkálẹ̀ ìròyìn nípa lílo àwọn àgbéjáde ìròyìn tí olú ilé iṣẹ́ orílẹ̀ èdè pèsè rẹ̀ tó wà ní ìpínlẹ̀ míràn. Irúfẹ́ ìgbésẹ̀ wo ni ó máa ṣe ìgbélárugẹ fún ànfààní tí ó ní láti ṣe àṣeyọrí pẹ̀lú àwọn ohun èèlò wọ̀nyí?

A
Lo àwọn àjọṣe tí ó ní pẹ̀lú àwọn afúnni ti ìbílẹ̀ láti jèrè ìtànkálẹ̀ ìròyìn.
B
Mú àwọn àgbéjáde ìròyìn náà láti jẹ́ ti èdè abínibí fún àwọn èèrò nínú àyíká rẹ.
✓ Correct
C
Pè àwọn àgbéjáde ìròyìn gẹ́gẹ́ bí a ti kọ wọ́n sílẹ̀, ṣùgbọ́n ṣàfikún ọ̀rọ̀ láti ọ̀dọ̀ olùdarí àgbà ti abala tí o wà.
D
Mú kí àwọn olóòtù rántí wípé ilé iṣẹ́ rẹ jẹ́ tí orílẹ̀ èdè, tí kìíṣe èyí tó wà fún èrè jíjẹ pàtàkì tí olùtẹ̀wéjáde wọn máa ńfún ní nnkan.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

Correct Answer: (B) Mú àwọn àgbéjáde ìròyìn náà láti jẹ́ ti èdè abínibí fún àwọn èèrò nínú àyíká rẹ. [Subject: Public Relations · Medium: EN].

Irúfẹ́ gbólóhùn wo ni ó jẹ́ òtítọ́ nípa ẹgbẹ́ àfojúsùn ìwádìí?

A
Àwọn olùkópa ni kí á yàn ní àìlétò.
B
Ó jẹ́ àpẹẹrẹ irinṣẹ́ ìwádìí ti ọ́fíìsì fún àwọn olùkópa.
C
Àlàyé náà ṣeé sọ di gbogboogbò láti ṣe àpèjúwe iye àwọn ènìyàn tó pọ̀.
D
Àwọn àbájáde ń ṣe àmúwá dáta tó yanrantí tí a lè lò láti fi mú ìdàgbàsókè bá àwọn ìwádìí
✓ Correct
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option D)

Correct Answer: (D) Àwọn àbájáde ń ṣe àmúwá dáta tó yanrantí tí a lè lò láti fi mú ìdàgbàsókè bá àwọn ìwádìí [Subject: Public Relations · Medium: EN].

Èwo nínú àwọn gbólóhùn tó wà nísàlẹ̀ yìí ló ṣàpèjúwe ojúṣe àwọn olùṣèwádìí nípa lílo àwọn ẹranko nínú ìwádìí nípa ìrònú àti ìhùwàsí?

A
A ò gbọ́dọ̀ lo àwọn ẹranko fún ìwádìí nígbàkigbà tá a bá rí àwọn ọ̀nà mìíràn láti ṣe ìwádìí.
B
Kò yẹ kí wọ́n máa lo àwọn ẹranko fún ìwádìí tí ìwádìí náà bá máa kó wọn sí yọ́ọ́yọ́ọ́ tàbí kó ìdààmú bá wọn.
C
Ó lè jẹ́ pé bí àwọn ẹranko bá ní ìrora tàbí tí wọ́n bá ní ìdààmú ọkàn, ìyẹn lè mú kí wọ́n ṣe ìwádìí, àmọ́ kìkì ìgbà tí wọn kò bá rí ọ̀nà míì (tí kì í dunni) tí ìwádìí náà sì lè wúlò gan-an ni.
✓ Correct
D
Ó bófin mu láti máa dá àwọn ẹranko lóró tàbí láti máa kó wọn sí wàhálà nígbà tí wọ́n bá ń ṣe ìwádìí, àmọ́ kìkì tí wọ́n bá dín in kù, tí wọ́n sì pa wọ́n ní kíákíá bó bá ti lè ṣeé ṣe tó.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

Correct Answer: (C) Ó lè jẹ́ pé bí àwọn ẹranko bá ní ìrora tàbí tí wọ́n bá ní ìdààmú ọkàn, ìyẹn lè mú kí wọ́n ṣe ìwádìí, àmọ́ kìkì ìgbà tí wọn kò bá rí ọ̀nà míì (tí kì í dunni) tí ìwádìí náà sì lè wúlò gan-an ni. [Subject: Professional Psychology · Medium: EN].

A
rán olùjẹ́jọ́ náà létí pé kò sí ṣíṣe kò sí àìṣe níwọ̀n bí ó ti jẹ́ pé ilé ẹjọ́ ni ó pàṣẹ àyẹ̀wò náà.
B
kọ̀ láti ṣe àyẹ̀wò náà àyàfi tí ó bá gba fọ́ọ̀mù ìfọwọ́sí kan tí a bu ọwọ́ lù láti ọ̀dọ̀ olùjẹ́jọ́ náà.
C
kọ̀ láti se àyẹ̀wò náà àyàfi tí ó bá gba fọ́ọ̀mù ìfọwọ́sí kan tí a bu ọwọ́ lù láti ọ̀dọ̀ agbẹjọ́rò olùjẹ́jọ́ náà.
D
sún àyẹwò náà síwájú kí ó sì gba olùjẹ́jọ́ náà ní ìmọ̀ràn láti kàn sí agbẹjọ́rò rẹ̀.

Awọn obinrin meji ngbe ni ile iyẹwu kan pẹlu 20 aja. Ẹ̀rù ba ajá-ẹlẹ́sẹ̀ kan tí wọ́n wọ inú ilé náà nítorí ipò ìgbésí ayé wọn tó burú jáì. Ẹ̀jẹ̀ àwọn ẹran náà ni a kó sí gbogbo ilé náà, wọ́n kó wọn sínú àpótí kéékèèké, àwọn ẹranko kan sì ń ṣàìsàn. Alejo ti a npe ni SPCA, a ikọkọ ti kii-èrè awujo eda eniyan. Ofin ipinlẹ fun awọn awujọ omoniyan laṣẹ lati gba awọn ẹranko ti o ni ilokulo tabi ti a gbagbe lati ọdọ awọn oṣiṣẹ iṣakoso ẹranko, lati tọju wọn ati gba wọn jade. SPCA mu awọn ẹranko ti a yan nipasẹ awọn alaṣẹ. Awọn olori lẹhinna mu awọn obinrin fun aibikita ẹranko. Nígbà ìgbẹ́jọ́ kan, agbẹjọ́rò náà kò múra sílẹ̀, adájọ́ sì fagi lé ẹ̀sùn náà. Awọn obinrin lẹhinna fi ẹsun awọn oṣiṣẹ ati SPCA labẹ 42 U.S.C. Â§ 1983 fun awọn ẹtọ ilu. Awọn olujebi SPCA fi ẹsun kan lati yọ kuro da lori isansa ti “igbese ipinlẹ” ni apakan wọn. Njẹ ile-ẹjọ yoo jẹ ki o gba ẹjọ lati yọ kuro?

A
Bẹẹni, nitori ko si ohun ti o ṣe nipasẹ awujọ eniyan ti o kan igbese ọlọpa.
B
Bẹẹni, nitori awọn oṣiṣẹ SPCA ko ṣe ni eyikeyi akoko “labẹ awọ ofin” ninu awọn iṣe wọn.
C
Rara, nitori pe a ṣeto SPCA labẹ iwe-ẹri ti kii ṣe èrè ti isọdọkan ti ipinlẹ ti funni.
D
Rara, nitori SPCA ṣiṣẹ labẹ aṣẹ ti ofin ipinlẹ ati ifowosowopo pẹlu awọn alaṣẹ ipinlẹ lati ṣe awọn iṣẹ ti a fun ni aṣẹ.
✓ Correct
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option D)

Correct Answer: (D) Rara, nitori SPCA ṣiṣẹ labẹ aṣẹ ti ofin ipinlẹ ati ifowosowopo pẹlu awọn alaṣẹ ipinlẹ lati ṣe awọn iṣẹ ti a fun ni aṣẹ. [Subject: Professional Law · Medium: EN].

Lakoko awọn ọjọ ikẹhin ti Alakoso rẹ, ọpọlọpọ awọn ọmọ ẹgbẹ Igbimọ Alakoso bẹrẹ jijo kuku awọn itan didamu nipa Alakoso si awọn oniroyin. Ninu ọkan iru itan bẹẹ, eyiti o han ninu iwe iroyin kan, ọmọ ẹgbẹ minisita ti a ko mọ ni a sọ pe Alakoso dariji oniwun ti ẹgbẹ bọọlu afẹsẹgba kan, fun idalẹjọ ẹṣẹ ti iṣaaju nitori oluwa ti ṣe ileri lati fun Alakoso ni igbesi aye igbesi aye si gbogbo ọjọ iwaju. awọn ere. Ibinu nipasẹ awọn iroyin wọnyi “awọn n jo,” Alakoso, ninu iṣe aṣẹ rẹ ti o kẹhin, ti paṣẹ aṣẹ alaṣẹ ti o fi ofin de gbogbo awọn oṣiṣẹ alaṣẹ lati ni ibaraẹnisọrọ pẹlu awọn ọmọ ẹgbẹ ti atẹjade ayafi ti igbanilaaye iṣaaju ti gba lati ọdọ alabojuto iṣakoso kan. Aṣẹ alaṣẹ pe fun yiyọ kuro ti oṣiṣẹ eyikeyi ti o mọọmọ ni ilodi si aṣẹ yẹn. Lẹhin ti Alakoso ti lọ kuro ni ọfiisi, awọn atẹjade ṣe awọn itọkasi leralera si aṣẹ alaṣẹ ati pe o tọka si i bi ogún ti Alakoso. Sibẹsibẹ, lẹhin ti Aare titun ti gba ọfiisi o ṣe ileri lati tẹle aṣẹ naa. Lẹ́yìn náà, ọ̀kan lára ​​àwọn olùdámọ̀ràn àgbà fún Igbakeji Ààrẹ, jo ìtàn kan sí ìwé ìròyìn kan tí ó sọ pé Igbákejì Ààrẹ sọ fún àwọn òṣìṣẹ́ rẹ̀ pé ìdí kan ṣoṣo tí ó fi ṣèbẹ̀wò sí ìlú kan lẹ́yìn ìmìtìtì ilẹ̀ kan ni nítorí pé ó fẹ́ ṣe gọ́ọ̀bù ní ibi ìdánilẹ́kọ̀ọ́ gọ́ọ̀bù kan nítòsí. Iwe irohin naa tẹjade itan naa o si sọ agba agba-imọran gẹgẹbi orisun. Ni ibamu pẹlu awọn ilana iṣẹ ilu, igbọran kan ni a ṣe ati pe oludamoran agba ni a rii pe o ti ṣẹ aṣẹ alase ti o dena awọn ibaraẹnisọrọ laigba aṣẹ pẹlu media titẹjade. Lẹhinna, oludamoran agba ti yọkuro ni ṣoki lati ipo oṣiṣẹ alaṣẹ rẹ. Oludamọran agba mu igbese ti o yẹ wa ti n wa imupadabọ ati koju ofin t’olofin ti aṣẹ alaṣẹ. Ti ẹjọ yii ba de ile-ẹjọ giga ti AMẸRIKA, awọn onidajọ yẹ ki o ṣe akoso aṣẹ alase jẹ

A
t'olofin, nitori oojọ ti ijọba jẹ anfani ati kii ṣe ẹtọ.
B
t'olofin, nitori Aare ni agbara gbogbo lati ṣeto awọn ilana iṣẹ fun awọn oṣiṣẹ ẹka alaṣẹ.

aiṣedeede, nitori Ile asofin ijoba, kii ṣe Alakoso, ni aṣẹ lati ṣe ilana awọn ofin ati ipo ti oojọ apapo.
D
alaigbagbọ, nitori Aare ko le fi opin si gbogbo ominira ti ọrọ-ọrọ ati ajọṣepọ ti gbogbo awọn oṣiṣẹ alaṣẹ.

#248545 · SUBLAWCONS_Q509106 DB1_SCRAPED_MODIFIED UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_LAW_CONST — Constitutional & Administrative Law Medium PYQ: Global & Regional Medium Assessment (EN) ()Body: Apache-2.0 Global Academic Bank · India / Global

Èwo nínú àwọn ìṣẹ̀lẹ̀ wọ̀nyí ni ó ṣeé ṣe kí ìwà olùjẹ́jọ́ mú un jẹ́ ẹni tó jẹ̀bi ẹ̀sùn ọ̀daràn fún ikú olùfaragbà.

A
Olùjẹ́jọ́ kan yín ìbọn fún olùfaragbà lórí. Wọ́n gbé olùfaragbà náà lọ sí ilé-ìwòsàn fún ìtọ́jú ọgbẹ́ náà. Lẹ́yìn náà Ilẹ̀ ríri kan kọlu ìlú náà, tí ó jẹ́ kí ilé ìwòsàn náà wó lulẹ̀. Olùfaragbà náà ni àwókù ilé náà wó pa.
B
Olùjẹ́jọ́ kan àti olùfaragbà kan jọ ń gbé nínú ilé kan. Olùjẹ́jọ́ náà mọ̀ wípé olùfaragbà náà ń ní àjọṣepọ̀ ìfẹ́ pẹ̀lú obìnrin tó ti gbéyàwó. Ní ọjọ́ kan, olùjẹ́jọ́ náà gbọ́ wípé ọkọ obìnrin yìí fẹ́ pa olùfaragbà náà. Bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ wípé olùjẹ́jọ́ náà ní àkókò tó pọ̀ láti kìlọ̀ fún olùfaragbà náà, ó kọ̀ láti ṣe bẹ́ẹ̀. Ní alẹ́ ọjọ́ náà, ọkọ arábìnrin yìí fi ọ̀bẹ gùn olùfaragba náà pa.
C
Olùfaragbà kan, tí ó jẹ́ atatẹ́tẹ́, jẹ́ àwọn jàǹdùkú ní gbèsè tó pọ̀ gan-an. Olùjẹ́jọ́ kan, tó jẹ́ ògbóǹkangí àwọn jàǹdùkú, ń halẹ̀ mọ́ ọkùnrin náà pé òun yóò pá tí kò bá san owó tó jẹ́. Pẹ̀lú ìbẹ̀rù, olùfaragbà náà wọ ọkọ̀ òfuurufú to kan kúrò ní ìlú. Ọkọ̀ òfuurufú náà já, ó sì pa olùfaragbà náà.
D
Olùjẹ́jọ́ kan àti olùfaragbà kan jọ ń wa ọkọ̀ papọ̀ lọ sí ibi iṣẹ́ nígbà tí olùjẹ́jọ́ náà, fi ọbẹ̀ gún un ní apá láì ṣe ohunkóhun láti bíi nínú. Olùjẹ́jọ́ náà tì í jáde kúrò nínú ọkọ̀ ayọ́kẹ́lẹ́ náà. Olùfaragbà náà ṣubú sí ẹ̀gbẹ́ ọ̀nà ó sì fọ́ ní kókósẹ̀. Nítorí pé kò lè dìde kúrò láàárín ọ̀nà, ọkọ̀ ayọ́kẹ́lẹ́ kan tí awakọ̀ tó ti mutí yó kan ń wà tẹ olùfaragbà náà pa ní ǹkan bíi ọgbọ̀n-ìṣẹ́jú lẹ́yìn náà.
✓ Correct
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option D)

Correct Answer: (D) Olùjẹ́jọ́ kan àti olùfaragbà kan jọ ń wa ọkọ̀ papọ̀ lọ sí ibi iṣẹ́ nígbà tí olùjẹ́jọ́ náà, fi ọbẹ̀ gún un ní apá láì ṣe ohunkóhun láti bíi nínú. Olùjẹ́jọ́ náà tì í jáde kúrò nínú ọkọ̀ ayọ́kẹ́lẹ́ náà. Olùfaragbà náà ṣubú sí ẹ̀gbẹ́ ọ̀nà ó sì fọ́ ní kókósẹ̀. Nítorí pé kò lè dìde kúrò láàárín ọ̀nà, ọkọ̀ ayọ́kẹ́lẹ́ kan tí awakọ̀ tó ti mutí yó kan ń wà tẹ olùfaragbà náà pa ní ǹkan bíi ọgbọ̀n-ìṣẹ́jú lẹ́yìn náà. [Subject: Professional Law · Medium: EN].

#248544 · SUBLAWCONS_Q509105 DB1_SCRAPED_MODIFIED UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_LAW_CONST — Constitutional & Administrative Law Medium PYQ: Global & Regional Medium Assessment (EN) ()Body: Apache-2.0 Global Academic Bank · India / Global

Ọkùnrin kan wá gba ayàwòrán fídíò síṣẹ́ láti ya fídíò ìgbéyàwó ọmọbìnrin rẹ̀. Ìwé àdéhùn tí wọ́n kọ sínú ìwé náà ní "ọ̀rọ̀ kan nínú tó sọ pé wọ́n máa "san 10,000 fún fídíò ìgbéyàwó náà àti àpèjẹ náà fún odindi ìṣẹ́jú 60.” Ọkùnrin náà fi kún ìwé àdéhùn náà pé àwọn ohun èlò tó ní ìtumọ̀ gíga ló máa fi ṣe fídíò náà. Àdéhùn náà kò mẹ́nu kan àwọn nǹkan mìíràn tó ní í ṣe pẹ̀lú owó ìtaràn. Lẹ́yìn náà, ayàwòrán náà ya fídíò náà tó gùn tó ìṣẹ́jú 60, ó sì tún un ṣe, ó lo àwọn ohun èlò tó ń mú kí fídíò náà ṣe kedere, ó sì fi hàn án fún ọkùnrin náà. Lẹ́yìn náà, ayàwòrán fídíò náà fún ọkùnrin náà ní ẹ̀rí owó tó jẹ́ 15,000. Yàtọ̀ sí iye tí wọ́n san fún iṣẹ́ náà, ìyẹn 10,000, owó tí wọ́n fi gba àwọn ẹ̀rọ gíga fíìmù náà jẹ́ 5,000. Èwo nínú àwọn àríyànjiyàn tó wà nísàlẹ̀ yìí ló ṣeé ṣe jù lọ láti tì ayàwòrán lẹ́yìn nínú àlàyé rẹ̀ pé nígbà tí ìwé àdéhùn tí wọ́n kọ sílẹ̀ náà bá wáyé, ọkùnrin náà gbà láti san 5,000 fún ayàwòrán fídíò fún lílo àwọn ohun èlò gíga-ìtumọ̀ láfikún sí owó 10,000?

A
Níbàámu pẹ̀lú àṣà ìṣòwò tó wọ́pọ̀ lágbo iṣẹ́ fídíò, owó tí wọ́n máa ń san fún fídíò yíyà tó 10,000 túmọ̀ sí pé wọ́n á san 10,000 lẹ́yìn owó àfikún tí wọ́n máa ń san bí wọ́n bá lo àwọn ẹ̀rọ tó ń gbé àwòrán jáde lọ́nà tó ṣe kedere.
✓ Correct
B
Àdéhùn tí wọ́n bá ṣe ní ẹnu, tí wọ́n bá lè fi hàn pé ó wà níbàámu, yóò wulẹ̀ jẹ́ àfikún, kì í ṣe àtakò sí "àlàkalẹ̀ ìsanwó" tí wọ́n kọ.
C
Lábẹ́ àwọn òfin tó ń ṣàkóso káràkátá ọjà ní orílẹ̀-èdè Amẹ́ríkà, tí àwọn ẹ̀rí tí kò fojú hàn bá wà, àwọn àlàyé míràn tí ẹgbẹ́ méjèèjì jọ fara mọ́ jẹ́ ìtẹ́wọ́gbà àyàfi tí àwọn àlàyé náà “bá yàtọ̀ tàbí lòdì sí àwọn èyí tó wà nínú àdéhùn àkọsílẹ̀ náà.”
D
Nítorí àríyànjiyàn wípé “àkọsílẹ̀ nípa bí a ṣe ń sanwó” jẹ́ síso papọ̀ tí kò sì hàn gbangba pé kò ṣe kedere tàbí ohun tí ó ṣe kedere, ohun tí ó yẹ kí wọ́n ṣe pèsè fún lílo àwọn ẹ̀rí tí kò fojú hàn tí wọ́n bá wà fún àǹfààní àwọn ẹ̀gbẹ́ méjèèjì tí ọ̀rọ̀ náà kan, nígbà tí ó jẹ́ wípé olùyafídíò náà yóò pàdánù ẹgbẹ̀rún márùn-ún dọ́là (USD 5,000) lórí àdéhùn àkọsílẹ̀ náà.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

Correct Answer: (A) Níbàámu pẹ̀lú àṣà ìṣòwò tó wọ́pọ̀ lágbo iṣẹ́ fídíò, owó tí wọ́n máa ń san fún fídíò yíyà tó 10,000 túmọ̀ sí pé wọ́n á san 10,000 lẹ́yìn owó àfikún tí wọ́n máa ń san bí wọ́n bá lo àwọn ẹ̀rọ tó ń gbé àwòrán jáde lọ́nà tó ṣe kedere. [Subject: Professional Law · Medium: EN].

#248543 · SUBLAWCONS_Q509104 DB1_SCRAPED_MODIFIED UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_LAW_CONST — Constitutional & Administrative Law Medium PYQ: Global & Regional Medium Assessment (EN) ()Body: Apache-2.0 Global Academic Bank · India / Global

Ní ọjọ́ ìṣẹ́gun, onílé kan ra ohun ẹ̀rọ ìgéko tuntun kan ní ilé ìtajà. Ní ọjọ́ kejì, ó ń gé koríko ní ẹ̀yìn ilé rẹ̀ pẹ̀lú ẹ̀rọ náà nígbà tí òjò bẹ̀rẹ̀ sí ní rọ̀. Ó dẹkùn lílo ẹ̀rọ náà o sì pa. Ó wọ inú ilé lọ, ó sì dúró kí òjò náà dá kí ó tó tẹ̀síwájú láti gé àwọn koríko tó kù. Lẹ́yìn ìṣẹ́jú díẹ̀ nígbà tí onílé náà wà nínú ilé rẹ̀, lójijì ẹ̀rọ ìgéko náà lọ síwájú láìsí fúnra rẹ̀, ó yí já bọ́ láti òkè kan, ó sì wọ ilẹ̀ aládùúgbò rẹ̀. Ẹ̀rọ náà gé àwọn igi róòsì to ṣe iyebíye, ó sì bà wọ́n jẹ́. Lẹ́yìn náà ni wọ́n rí wípé ohun tí wọ́n fi ń tan ẹ̀rọ náà ti bàjẹ́. Bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ pé onílé náà tí ti fi ohun tí wọ́n fi ń pa ẹ̀rọ náà sì ipò pípa, síbẹ̀ ẹ̀rọ náà sì wà ní títàn. Nítorí wípé ẹ̀rọ tó ń yípo tí kò ní ariwo ni wọ́n fi ṣe ẹ̀rọ ìgéko náà, kò sì ìdí tí onílé náà yó fi mọ̀ pé ẹ̀rọ náà ń ṣiṣẹ́ lọ nígbà tí òun wọ inú ilé. Kò sí bí onílé náà yóò ṣe mọ̀ pé ẹ̀rọ náà ní àléébù nípa ṣíṣe àyẹ̀wò. Tí aládùúgbò náà bá fi ẹ̀sùn ìkọjá àyè kan ọkùnrin onílé náà, ṣe aládùúgbò náà yóò borí?

A
Bẹ́ẹ̀ ni, nítorí pé ẹ̀rọ ìgéko náà wọ inú ilẹ̀ aládùúgbò náà.
B
Bẹ́ẹ̀ ni, nítorí pé onílé náà ní ẹ̀tọ́ láti sanwó fún ìpalára tí ẹ̀rọ tó bàjẹ́ náà fà.
C
Rárá, nítorí pé ẹni tó ni ilé náà ń lo ẹ̀rọ ìgéko náà fún ète tí wọ́n yàn fún un.
D
Rárá, nítorí pé wọn kò ṣàwárí ohun tí wọ́n fi ń pá ẹ̀rọ náà, èyí tó ti bàjẹ́, lára ẹ̀rọ nípa ṣíṣe àyẹ̀wò.
✓ Correct
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option D)

Correct Answer: (D) Rárá, nítorí pé wọn kò ṣàwárí ohun tí wọ́n fi ń pá ẹ̀rọ náà, èyí tó ti bàjẹ́, lára ẹ̀rọ nípa ṣíṣe àyẹ̀wò. [Subject: Professional Law · Medium: EN].

Kí ni wọ́n parí nínú ìwádìí kan lẹ́nu àìpẹ́ yìí tí ó ń ṣe àfiwé àkójọpọ̀ àwọn ènìyàn òde òní pẹ̀lú àwọn òkú egungun àtijọ́ ní Àríwá àti Gúúsù Amẹ́ríkà?

A
Àwọn ọmọ ìbílẹ̀ Amẹ́ríkà ní Àríwá Amẹ́ríkà àti Gúúsù Amẹ́ríkà yàtọ̀ sí ara wọn àti àwọn baba ńlá wọn ní àríwá ní nkan bí ẹgbẹ̀rún lọ́nà 30,000 ọdún sẹ́yìn.
B
Gbogbo àwọn ọmọ ìbílẹ̀ Amẹ́ríkà, àtijọ́ àti ìgbàlódé, ni wọ́n wá láti ìṣípòpọ̀ austral-Melanesians kan ṣoṣo sí Amẹ́ríkà ní nkan bí ọdún 13,000 sẹ́yìn.
C
Gbogbo àwọn ọmọ ìbílẹ̀ Amẹ́ríkà, àtijọ́ àti ìgbàlódé, ni wọ́n wá láti inú ìṣípòpọ̀ kan ṣoṣo ti Inuit, tí wọ́n dé Amẹ́ríkà ní nkan bí ọdún 13,000 sẹ́yìn.
D

✓ Correct

#248578 · SUBBTBIOCH_Q713940 DB2_PYQ UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_BT_BIOCHEM_MICRO — Biochemistry & General Microbiology (GATE BT) Medium PYQ: GATE BT (2021)Body: IIT-IISc · India
Domain: 1. Engineering & Technology · Unit: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Glycolysis, Gluconeogenesis, Pentose Phosphate Pathway · Topic: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Citric Acid (TCA) Cycle, Glyoxylate Cycle, Oxidative Phosphorylation & Ele

Which enzyme of the citric acid cycle is also a component of the mitochondrial electron transport chain?

A
Succinate dehydrogenase
✓ Correct
B
Aconitase
C
Fumarase
D
Malate dehydrogenase
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

Succinate dehydrogenase catalyzes oxidation of succinate to fumarate in the TCA cycle and also functions as Complex II, transferring electrons from FADH2 to ubiquinone. Aconitase, fumarase, and malate dehydrogenase are TCA cycle enzymes but are not components of the electron transport chain. Therefore, option A is correct.

Which complex of the mitochondrial electron transport chain does not pump protons from the matrix to the intermembrane space?

A
Complex I
B
Complex II
✓ Correct
C
Complex III
D
Complex IV
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

Complex I, Complex III, and Complex IV are proton pumps that contribute to the proton-motive force. Complex II, succinate dehydrogenase, accepts electrons from FADH2 and passes them to ubiquinone, but its free-energy change is insufficient for proton translocation. Therefore, Complex II is the correct answer.

📖 Shared Reading Comprehension Passage (high19804) Shared ID: PSG_3F6E59A1E41D · Question 2 of 2

Two new studies have investigated why fewer women, compared to men, study and work in the so-called STEM subjects in the United States: science, technology, engineering and mathematics.

The American Association of University Women(AAUW) examined existing research. Its report "Why So Few?" suggested ways to interest more girls and women in the STEM fields. The researchers found that cultural and environmental factors make a difference.

Researcher Christianne Corbett says more boys than girls score very high on math tests in most countries, but Iceland and Thailand are exceptions. "This is something that we point out in our report--that cultural factors and societal factors can make a difference in who achieves at the very high levels and whether girls are achieving or not."

Another study carried out by the Campos Company for the Bayer Corporation in the United States asked more than 1,000 women and minority members of the American Chemical Society about their experiences.77% said not enough women and minorities are working in STEM today, because they were not encouraged to study those subjects in school.

The study, producing results similar to the AAUW research showed there's still a lot of work to be done. "They say their interest in science begins before the age of eleven. So we need programs that get in front of kids while they're young in elementary school."

Increasing diversity in professions leads to better products, better science. And developing this science and engineering workforce is important to the nation's economic development.

"No matter what career you go into, whether it's accounting, human resources or science career, it's important that you can think creatively, you can work in teams, you can adapt to change and that's important for everybody," says Rebecca Lucore.

Which of the following are mentioned as important factors for women to go into the STEM fields according to the passage?

A
Gender and age.
B
Culture and environment.
✓ Correct
C
Study and work experience.
D
Family background.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

The passage states that researchers found cultural and environmental factors make a difference for women entering STEM. Therefore option B is correct. Options A, C, and D mention gender and age, study and work experience, or family background, but the visible passage does not identify those as the important factors.

<<<<<<< Updated upstream #248576 · SUBBTBIOCH_Q713938 DB1_SCRAPED_MODIFIED UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_BT_BIOCHEM_MICRO — Biochemistry & General Microbiology (GATE BT) Medium PYQ: N/A (N/A)Body: N/A · N/A
Domain: 1. Engineering & Technology · Unit: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Glycolysis, Gluconeogenesis, Pentose Phosphate Pathway · Topic: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Citric Acid (TCA) Cycle, Glyoxylate Cycle, Oxidative Phosphorylation & Ele

Why does the glyoxylate cycle permit net synthesis of carbohydrate from acetyl-CoA, unlike the standard citric acid cycle?

A
It bypasses both decarboxylation reactions that release CO2 in the TCA cycle.
✓ Correct
B
It converts every acetyl-CoA directly into glucose without intermediates.
C
It generates more NADH than the TCA cycle per acetyl-CoA.
D
It replaces succinate dehydrogenase with a proton-pumping complex.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

The glyoxylate cycle uses isocitrate lyase and malate synthase to convert two acetyl-CoA molecules into succinate while avoiding the CO2-releasing steps catalyzed by isocitrate dehydrogenase and alpha-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase. This preserves carbon for gluconeogenesis. It does not convert acetyl-CoA directly to glucose, does not primarily increase NADH yield, and does not replace succinate dehydrogenase with a proton pump. Thus, A is correct.

#248575 · SUBBTBIOCH_Q713937 DB1_SCRAPED_MODIFIED 🖼️ Figure / Diagram UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_BT_BIOCHEM_MICRO — Biochemistry & General Microbiology (GATE BT) Medium PYQ: N/A (N/A)Body: N/A · N/A
Domain: 1. Engineering & Technology · Unit: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Glycolysis, Gluconeogenesis, Pentose Phosphate Pathway · Topic: Metabolic Pathways & Bioenergetics: Citric Acid (TCA) Cycle, Glyoxylate Cycle, Oxidative Phosphorylation & Ele

In the citric acid cycle, which enzyme catalyzes the oxidative decarboxylation of isocitrate to alpha-ketoglutarate?

A
Isocitrate dehydrogenase
✓ Correct
B
Alpha-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase
C
Succinate dehydrogenase
D
Malate dehydrogenase
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

Isocitrate dehydrogenase oxidizes isocitrate and releases CO2, forming alpha-ketoglutarate and NADH. Alpha-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase acts on the next intermediate, not isocitrate. Succinate dehydrogenase oxidizes succinate to fumarate and does not decarboxylate. Malate dehydrogenase regenerates oxaloacetate from malate. Therefore, option A is correct.

#248574 · SUBGSGK_Q711596 DB2_PYQ UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_GS_GK — Multidisciplinary Competitive Exam & General Studies Medium PYQ: Global & National Competitive Exam (ceval-exam) (2021-2024)Body: Official Academic & Professional Exam Benchmark · ALL_COUNTRIES
Domain: Competitive Exams · Unit: Probability And Statistics · Topic: Probability And Statistics (ZH Exam Paper)

设二维随机变量(X,Y)的概率密度函数为:f(x,y)={a(x+y),0<x<2,0<y<10,其他,则常数a=____

A
13
✓ Correct
B
2
C
3
D
12
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

1. 首先，对于二维随机变量(X,Y)，其概率密度函数f(x,y)需满足归一性，即∬−∞∞f(x,y)dxdy=1。 2. 其次，我们需要计算概率密度函数在定义域内的二重积分。根据题目给定的定义域，我们有：∬02∬01a(x+y)dydx。 3. 接着，我们计算二重积分。首先对y积分，得到∫01a(x+y)dy=a[x+12(y2)]01=a(x+12)。然后对x积分，得到∫02a(x+12)dx=a[12(x2+x)]02=3a。 4. 最后，根据归一性，我们有3a=1，解得常数a=13。

#248573 · SUBGSGK_Q711594 DB2_PYQ UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_GS_GK — Multidisciplinary Competitive Exam & General Studies Medium PYQ: Global & National Competitive Exam (ceval-exam) (2021-2024)Body: Official Academic & Professional Exam Benchmark · ALL_COUNTRIES
Domain: Competitive Exams · Unit: Probability And Statistics · Topic: Probability And Statistics (ZH Exam Paper)

独立地测量一个物理量,记每次测量的结果为X=μ+ε,其中μ是物理量的真值,ε是测量产生的随机误差,且已知每次测量产生的随机误差都服从(−1,1)上的均匀分布,如果取n次测量结果的算术平均值X¯=164∑i=164Xi作为真值μ的近似值,用切比雪夫不等式估计|X¯−μ|<18的概率的结果:____

A
不超过 23
B
不小于 13
C
不小于 23
✓ Correct
D
不超过 13
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

1. 首先，我们需要知道切比雪夫不等式：对于任意随机变量X，其期望为μ，方差为σ^2，那么对于任意正数k，有P(|X−μ|≥kσ)≤1k2。 2. 其次，我们需要计算随机误差ε的期望和方差。由题意知，ε服从(−1,1)上的均匀分布，其期望E(ε)=0，方差Var(ε)=2212=13。 3. 接着，我们计算X¯的期望和方差。由于每次测量都是独立的，所以E(X¯)=E(164∑i=164Xi)=μ，Var(X¯)=Var(164∑i=164Xi)=1642∑i=164Var(Xi)=164·13。 4. 最后，我们利用切比雪夫不等式估计P(|X¯−μ|<18)。由切比雪夫不等式，我们有P(|X¯−μ|≥18)≤Var(X¯)(18)2=164·13(18)2=13。所以，P(|X¯−μ|<18)≥1−13=23。 因此，|X¯−μ|<18的概率不小于23，答案是C。

Two new studies have investigated why fewer women, compared to men, study and work in the so-called STEM subjects in the United States: science, technology, engineering and mathematics.

The American Association of University Women(AAUW) examined existing research. Its report "Why So Few?" suggested ways to interest more girls and women in the STEM fields. The researchers found that cultural and environmental factors make a difference.

A
Boys score higher in math tests than girls.
B
Girls are given more exceptional attention than boys.
C
More girls score higher in math than boys.
✓ Correct
D
Boys are more creative than girls.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

Option A describes the usual pattern rather than the exception. Options B and D introduce unsupported claims about attention or creativity that the passage never mentions.

设随机变量(X,Y)的联合密度函数是:f(x,y)={ke−x−2y,x>0,y>00,其他则P(0≤X≤2,0≤Y≤1)=____

(1−e−4)2
B
(1−e−8)2

(1−e−2)2

(1−e−6)2

1. 首先，我们需要找到常数k。为了使f(x, y)成为一个有效的联合密度函数，我们需要满足∫−∞∞∫−∞∞f(x,y)dxdy=1。由于f(x, y)在x>0和y>0的区域内为非零值，我们可以将积分限改为∫0∞∫0∞ke−x−2ydxdy=1。 2. 计算积分，得到k∫0∞∫0∞e−x−2ydxdy=k∫0∞(−e−x−2y|x=0x=∞)dy=k∫0∞e−2ydy=k(−12e−2y|y=0y=∞)=k12=1。解得k=2。 3. 现在我们已经知道联合密度函数f(x,y)={2e−x−2y,x>0,y>00,其他。接下来，我们需要计算P(0≤X≤2,0≤Y≤1)，即在给定范围内的联合密度函数的积分：P(0≤X≤2,0≤Y≤1)=∫01∫022e−x−2ydxdy。 4. 计算积分，得到P(0≤X≤2,0≤Y≤1)=∫01(−e−x−2y|x=0x=2)dy=∫01(e−2y−e−2−2y)dy=(−12e−2y|y=0y=1)+12(e−2−2y|y=0y=1)=(1−e−2)2。

已知关于x的不等式组{x<3a+2,2x−4>0,恰有两个整数解，则a的取值范围为____

A
2≤a≤3
B
23<a≤1
✓ Correct
C
2<a≤3
D
23≤a<1
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

1. 首先，我们需要解第二个不等式2x−4>0。将其化简得到x>2。 2. 接着，我们将两个不等式组合在一起，得到x>2且x<3a+2。由题意，这个不等式组恰有两个整数解，因此，x=3和x=4。 3. 最后，我们需要找到a的取值范围。将这两个x取值代入不等式x<3a+2，得到3<3a+2和4<3a+2。解这两个不等式，我们得到a>13和a>23，合并两个条件得到a>23。由于题目要求恰有两个整数解，因此，x<5。所以有x<3a+2≤5，化简后得到a≤1。因此，a的取值范围为23<a≤1。

Hundreds of armed police have been deployed around the temple. Meanwhile, a debate about how to use the money has also broken out across the country. After discovering the treasure trove in Sri Padmanabhaswamy Temple in the southern Indian state of Kerala, the local government has assured security of the temple.

Elaborating on the security arrangements, Venugopalan Nair, Additional Director General of the Kerala Police, says the latest security devices are being used to guard the temple.

G. Jayasekharan Nair, administrative officer, said, "To control the crowds the government itself is taking a lot of measures. They are trying to do whatever is possible from the premises of the temple. Apart from all that, the government has taken a favorable step and we are satisfied with them."

As estimates of the treasures' worth rise, a fierce debate is growing regarding what to do with the discovery. A
x=0000007FH，y=FFF9H，z=00000076H
B
x=0000007FH，y=FFF9H，z=FFFF0076H

View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option D)

光子能量为0.5MeV的X射线，入射到某种物质上而发生康普顿散射．若反冲电子的能量为0.1MeV，则散射光波长的改变量Δλ与入射光波长λ_0之比值为____

A
0.2
B
0.25
✓ Correct
C
0.3
D
0.35
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

1. 首先，我们需要将入射光子能量和散射光子能量转换成波长。可以使用公式E=h*c/λ，其中E表示能量，λ表示波长。入射光子能量为0.5 MeV，对应波长λ0=h*c/(0.5MeV)。反冲电子能量为0.1 MeV，因此散射光子能量为0.4 MeV，对应波长λ′=h*c/(0.4MeV)。 2. 其次，我们计算Δλ=λ′−λ0=h*c/(0.4MeV)−h*c/(0.5MeV)。 3. 最后，我们要求(Δλ/λ0)=[(h*c/(0.4MeV)−h*c/(0.5MeV))/(h*c/(0.5MeV))]。化简得到比值为0.25。

有一劲度系数为k的轻弹簧，竖直放置，下端悬一质量为m的小球，开始时使弹簧为原长而小球恰好与地接触，今将弹簧上端缓慢地提起，直到小球刚能脱离地面为止，在此过程中外力作功为____

m2g2/(4k)
B
m2g2/(3k)

m2g2/(2k)

D
2m2g2/k
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

已知一垄断企业成本函数为TC=5Q2+20Q+10，产品的需求函数为Q=140-P。利润最大化的产量为____。

A
10
✓ Correct
B
5
C
3
D
15
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

1. 首先，垄断企业的利润最大化产量是当边际收益MR等于边际成本MC时。因此，我们需要求出该企业的边际收益和边际成本函数。 2. 其次，求出总收益TR。TR=P*Q=140Q−Q2。 3. 接着，通过总收益TR求出边际收益MR函数，即MR=ΔTRΔQ=140−2Q。 4. 最后，通过成本函数TC求出边际成本MC函数，即MC=ΔTCΔQ=20+10Q。 5. 根据上述计算，我们将边际收益函数和边际成本函数相等，即140−2Q=20+10Q，解得Q=10。因此，利润最大化的产量为10。

-150
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

1. 首先，小型开放经济的净出口公式：NX = S - I，其中NX表示净出口，S表示储蓄，I表示投资。 2. 其次，根据国民账户恒等式，Y = C + I + G + NX，可以得到S = Y - C - G。将数据代入得到S = 150。 3. 接着，根据题目中的数据计算投资，I = 250 - 1000r*，其中r*表示世界利率。将r* = 5%代入得到I = 200。 4. 最后，计算净出口。将步骤2和步骤3的结果代入净出口公式，得到NX = S - I = 150 - 200 = -50。

下列物质加入电池负极溶液中，使Zn2+/Zn-H+/H2组成的原电池电动势增大的是____

A
ZnSO4固体
B
Zn粒
C
Na2S溶液
✓ Correct
D
Na2SO4固体
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

1. 首先，我们需要了解Zn2+/Zn-H+/H2组成的原电池，其中Zn2+/Zn是负极，H+/H2是正极。 2. 其次，增大正极的电极电势或降低负极的电极电势可使电池电动势增大。显然，降低负极氧化型的浓度能使负极的电极电势减小。 3. 分析选项： (A)ZnSO4固体，(B) Zn粒和(D)Na2SO4固体，加入后不会改变电池的还原反应和氧化反应，因此不会使电池电动势增大；(C) Na2S溶液，加入S2−使大部分Zn2+被沉淀下来，降低了Zn2+的浓度，负极电势减小。 4. 根据以上分析，加入Na2S溶液可以使Zn2+/Zn-H+/H2组成的原电池电动势增大。

I was dirty smelly hungry and some somewhere beneath all that, suntanned .It was the end of an Inter-Rail holiday.My body couldn't take any more punishment. My mind couldn't deal with any more foreign timetables, currencies or languages.

Ordinary beds are never the same once you've learnt to sleep, in the passage of a train, the rhythm rocking you into a deep sleep.

Nothing.

A
12
B
13
✓ Correct
C
14
D
15
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option B)

1. 首先，我们观察到积分中的2x−x2，可以首先考虑进行变换。有(1−x)2x−x2=(1−x)1−(1−x)2。 2. 其次，考虑使用换元积分法进行求解。令1−x=u⇒x=1−u⇒dx=d(1−u)=−du。 3. 接着，我们需要将积分区间进行变换。当x=0时，u=1；当x=1时，u=0，因此积分区间变为[1,0]。 4. 将换元后的x代入原式，得到：∫01(1−x)2x−x2dx=∫10u1−u2(−du)=∫01u1−u2du。 5. 再次使用换元积分法，令u=sint⇒du=d(sint)=costdt，当u=0时，t=π2；当u=1时，t=0，因此，积分区间变为[0,π2]。 6. 最后，将换元后的u代入第4步的表达式，得到∫01(1−x)2x−x2=∫01u1−u2du=∫0π2sint1−sin2t·costdt=∫0π2sintcos2tdt=13。

求极限：limn→∞(1−11+2)(1−11+2+3)⋯(1−11+2+⋯+n)=____

A
23
B
12
C
56
D
13
✓ Correct

Domain: Aptitude & Comprehension · Unit: Multi-Question Exam Reading Comprehension Passages · Topic: Senior Competitive Exam Comprehension
📖 Shared Reading Comprehension Passage (high12759) Shared ID: PSG_3D687E615A0C · Question 1 of 1

As people have accurately observed,smiles are absent from early photographs. In 1852,for instance,a girl sat for her Daguerrotype,her head slightly turned,giving the camera an unsmiling look. She is preserved forever as a very serious girl indeed. Charles Darwin,a loving and playful parent,looks frozen in photographs. Why did our ancestors,from unknown sitters for family portraits to the great and famous,because so sad in front of the camera?

The severity is everywhere in Victorian photographs. However,you don't have to look very long at these unsmiling old photos to see how incomplete the seemingly obvious answer is-that they are freezing their faces in order to keep still for the long exposure times. In Julia Margaret Cameron's Portrait of Tennyson,the poet dreams,his face a shadowed mask of genius. This is not simply a technique. It's an emotional choice.

People in the past did not go around in a continual state of sorrow. In fact,the Victorians had a sense of humor even about the darkest aspects of their society. Laughter was not just common in the past but accepted by society far more than it is today,from medieval carnivals to Georgian print shops,where people gathered to look at the latest funnies. Far from preventing festivals and fun,the Victorians,who invented photography,also created Christmas as a celebration as it is today. So the severity of people in the 19th-century photographs cannot be the evidence of generalized sadness. This was not a society in permanent desperation. Instead,the true answer has to do with attitudes to portraiture itself.

People who sat for early photographs understood it as a significant moment. Sitting for the camera was cheaper,quicker and meant that people who never had a chance to be painted could now be photographed; but people seemed to have taken it seriously in the same way they would be a painted portrait. Like a portrait painting,it was intended as a timeless record of a person.

To me those unsmiling people probably had as much fun as we do,if not more. But they felt no need to prove it with pictures. Instead,when whey sat for a photograph,they thought about time,death and memory. Perhaps we should stop smiling sometimes,too.

What is the author's attitude towards people not smiling in old photos?

A
Skeptical.
✓ Correct
B
Critical.
C
Neutral.
D
Positive.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

The passage notes smiles are absent from early photographs and questions why ancestors looked sad, then says the obvious explanation is incomplete. This skeptical tone challenges a simple conclusion, so 'Skeptical' is correct. 'Critical' overstates judgment, 'Neutral' ignores the questioning stance, and 'Positive' contradicts the problematizing wording.

求极限：limx→0tan(tanx)−sin(sinx)tanx−sinx=____

A
-1
B
0
C
2
✓ Correct
D
12
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

1. 首先，观察表达式，对上述表达式进行拆分，L=limx→0tan(tanx)−sin(sinx)tanx−sinx=limx→0tan(tanx)−tan(sinx)tanx−sinx+limx→0tan(sinx)−sin(sinx)tanx−sinx=L1+L2 2. 其次，计算L1。有命题：设F(x)在x=0的邻域内可导，且limx→0F′(x)=A；又设limx→0f(x)=limx→0g(x)=0，且对0<|x|<δ有f(x)≠g(x)成立，则：limx→0F[f(x)]−F(g(x))f(x)−g(x)=A。对于L1，令F(x)=tanx，f(x)=tanx，g(x)=sinx，F′(x)=sec2x，limx→0F′(x)=sec20=1。于是有L1=limx→0F′(x)=1。 3. 接着，计算L2。有命题：设limx→0F(x)x=1，函数f(x)，h(x)在x=0的邻域附近解析，h(x)≢0，则：limx→0f[F(x)]h(x)=limx→0f(x)h(x)。对于L2，令F(x)=sinx，f(x)=tanx，g(x)=sinx，h(x)=tanx−sinx，且limx→0F(x)x=limx→0sinxx=1，则有L2=limx→0f[F(x)]−g[F(x)]h(x)=limx→0tanx−sinxtanx−sinx=1。 4. 最后，L=L1+L2=1+1=2。

📖 Shared Reading Comprehension Passage (high5407) Shared ID: PSG_EECF27A743FA · Question 1 of 1

In the US and Britain, the slogan around colleges was "Save water. Shower with a friend." Now, Wuhan University has come up with another system for the campus bathhouse. It charges students for the amount of time in a shower. Before entering the bathhouse, students pay for the amount of time they want in the shower with cash or their student ID card. The clock starts ticking the minute the tape is turned on. It pauses when a button is pressed for soap. An integrated circuit (IC) card reader at each tap shows the time. No money, no water. The benefits of the new system can be seen with the old system, which charged 1 Yuan for each person regardless of time in the shower. The university used about 320 tons of water daily under the old system, but only 160 tons now.

Many students use the new system but opinions on it are divided. Some students say it is bad because bathing had become a sort of race. Many people using it for the first time are not sure how long they need to shower. Some might be embarrassed if their time is up and they're still covered in soap. They have to ask the bathhouse worker to help them buy extra time.

"It's a _ in the system that you can not buy extra time on the ID card," said Ren, a freshman in Wuhan University. The university is also considering some students' suggestions that they be allowed to pay after they've finished the shower. Not surprisingly, some are complaining about losing the hour shower. But many students say the move helps them develop a water-saving sense.

Without the time limits, most students tended to shower for 30 to an hour in the bathhouse.

Some even used the hot water to wash their clothes. "In my experience, 10--20 minutes is enough," said Dai Zhihua, a third-year student who usually takes 8 minutes.

A similar system has been installed in other universities. Shanghai Normal University introduced it at its Fengxiang Campus in September. The bathing fee there is 0.2 Yuan per minute. One male student responded by setting a record with a two-minute shower.

According to the passage, which of the following is NOT true?

A
Students buy the time of their showers.
B
The clock times the student's bathing except when the bather pauses for soap.
C
If money runs out, there will be no water.
D
Having finished bathing, the student has to pay for it.
✓ Correct
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option D)

The passage states that students pay before entering for the desired shower time, the clock starts when the tap turns on and pauses for soap, and no money means no water. Therefore D is false because payment happens before bathing, not after. Options A, B, and C are directly supported by the text.

📖 Shared Reading Comprehension Passage (high13352) Shared ID: PSG_D369D54A0CD2 · Question 2 of 2

My parents knew their wealth: each other, their six children, and their faith. They tried to live simple lives so that they could have time for what was most important.

They didn't busy themselves buying a bigger house, because that would mean working harder to pay the monthly mortgage ,working overtime or taking second job. Who would go running with me then? Who would read stories to me?

They didn't burden themselves with buying an expensive car because that would mean worrying about installment bills. Besides, walking to the shopping centre every Saturday afternoon with me gave my dad his needed exercise, and made me feel so special.

One of my heart's delights was seeing Dad and Mom in their bedroom at night, after our nightly family prayer. The lights were turned off, and I'd see the figure of my father seated on his old chair and Mom standing behind him, gently massaging his shoulders. I'd hear them talk about what happened during their day. Even as a child, I sensed their quiet pleasure in being together.

My question today: Could they have done this rich ritual each night and nourished their marriage if they had been busy paying for expensive clothes for themselves or their kids, or if they had been worrying about monthly bills for new hi-tech equipment? I don't think so. And I've made a choice: I don't want that of life either.

What did the parents regularly do with the author?

A
They wrote stories together.
B
They read newspapers together.
C
They regularly took exercise together.
✓ Correct
D
They went shopping together each day.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

The parents walked with the author to the shopping centre every Saturday afternoon; this walk gave the father needed exercise and made the author feel special. Therefore the best-supported choice is that they regularly took exercise together. They did not write stories together, read newspapers together, or go shopping every day; the text specifies Saturday afternoons, not daily trips.

Kini awọn okunfa ti ipanilaya?

A
Ìjọba ìjọba orílẹ̀-èdè àti ìfẹ́ orílẹ̀-èdè ń dènà àfààní fún ìpànìyàn: ìfẹ́ orílẹ̀-èdè ń pèsè agbára èrò láti ṣe ìṣọ̀kan ìyàtọ̀ ẹ̀yà, àti pé ìjọba tiwa-n-tiwa ń gba ààyè fún àlàyé àwọn ìkórìíra ní ọ̀nà tí ó dára. Láàárín àtúpalẹ̀ yìí àwọn ìpínlẹ̀ tí wọ́n ní ìwé-ẹ̀rí ìjọba ìjọba àti olùfẹ́ orílẹ̀-èdè tí kò lágbára lè pèsè àwọn ipò tí ó ń ṣe ìrànlọ́wọ́ fún ìdàgbàsókè ìpànìyàn.
B
Ìwà ìbàjẹ́ láàárín àwọn ẹgbẹ́ Mùsùlùmí ni wọ́n ṣe àgbékalẹ̀ rẹ̀ lórí àwọn ìlà ẹ̀yà tí ó tako ẹ̀sìn tí ó tẹ̀lé ìgbàlódé. Ó lè jẹ́ ohun ìyàlẹ́nu pé bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ pé wọ́n ṣètò ìpànìyàn ní àyíká ìdẹ́rùbà sí àwùjọ Mùsùlùmí ní Àárín Ìlà Oòrùn, ìdáhùn sí ìdẹ́rùbà ti yọrí sí àwọn ẹgbẹ́ ní Europe tí wọ́n ní ìdẹ́rùbà nípasẹ̀ ìfọwọ́sowọ́pọ̀ àwọn Mùsùlùmí lórí àwọn ìwà Ìwọ̀ Oòrùn.
C
Àwọn iṣẹ́ ọjà olówó-nkan àti ìyípadà ìmọ̀-ẹ̀rọ àti ìbánisọ̀rọ̀ ti pọ̀ sí i lórí àṣà àti àwùjọ ìbílẹ̀. Àìdọ́gba ìdàgbàsókè àti ìyàsọ́tọ̀ àwọn ẹ̀yà tàbí ètò ọrọ̀ ajé kan nínú àwọn ènìyàn, pẹ̀lú ìmọ̀ tí ó ń pọ̀ sí i nípa ìyàsọ́tọ̀ wọn ń pèsè ìdí fún ìbísí ìwà ipá.
✓ Correct
D
Awọn eto ijọba ti kii ṣe tiwantiwa ti o ṣe afihan nipasẹ eto aarin ti o lagbara ati awọn ọna ipaniyan ṣii aaye fun ibanujẹ ti o gbajumọ lati yipada si awọn iṣẹ apanilaya ti iṣiṣẹ pẹlu ero lati pari eto iṣelu. 📖 Shared Reading Comprehension Passage (high13352) Shared ID: PSG_D369D54A0CD2 · Question 1 of 2

My parents knew their wealth: each other, their six children, and their faith. They tried to live simple lives so that they could have time for what was most important.

They didn't busy themselves buying a bigger house, because that would mean working harder to pay the monthly mortgage ,working overtime or taking second job. Who would go running with me then? Who would read stories to me?

They didn't burden themselves with buying an expensive car because that would mean worrying about installment bills. Besides, walking to the shopping centre every Saturday afternoon with me gave my dad his needed exercise, and made me feel so special.

One of my heart's delights was seeing Dad and Mom in their bedroom at night, after our nightly family prayer. The lights were turned off, and I'd see the figure of my father seated on his old chair and Mom standing behind him, gently massaging his shoulders. I'd hear them talk about what happened during their day. Even as a child, I sensed their quiet pleasure in being together.

My question today: Could they have done this rich ritual each night and nourished their marriage if they had been busy paying for expensive clothes for themselves or their kids, or if they had been worrying about monthly bills for new hi-tech equipment? I don't think so. And I've made a choice: I don't want that of life either.

Which of the following is TRUE about the author's family?

A
There were six people in the family.
B
They decided to buy a bigger house.
C
They lived an easy and happy life.
✓ Correct
D
They had a second-hand car.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option C)

The text says the parents valued family, faith, and simplicity, avoiding a bigger house and an expensive car so they could spend time with their children. The author felt special during Saturday walks. This supports “They lived an easy and happy life” in the intended sense, while six people is wrong because there were two parents plus six children, and buying a bigger house or second-hand car is not stated.

📖 Shared Reading Comprehension Passage (high7124) Shared ID: PSG_4C066A257A18 · Question 1 of 1

Educating Girls Is a Real Lifesaver

Clare Short knows it. Every developing economist knows it. The World Bank knows it. The education of girls is the surest way to reduce poverty.

The reason is simple. All the evidence shows that taking girls out of the fields and homes, and putting them behind desks, raises economic productivity, lowers infant and maternal death rates, reduces birth rates, and improves environmental management.

Why, then, are 90 million primary school-age girls around the world not in school? For the same reason that when Charles Dickens was writing David Copperfield 150 years ago girls were absent from the British education system: Men in power mostly prefer _ that way, or are not interested enough in changing the situation to commit energy and money to doing so.

The countries with the poorest record for having women in positions of power or influence have the worst figures for girls' education. High-profile intervention by organizations such as the World Bank has begun successfully with several countries, and more of the same will probably be needed to bring change in conservative, male-run states.

Even if there were no development payoff from gender equality in schools, the education of girls would still be a cause worth fighting for. Education is a human right, and the denial of it to girls is a scar on the community in the twenty-first century.

To be born a girl in a rural area in Nepal, Pakistan, Indonesia, Morocco, Togo, or Sudan -- half a dozen of the most shameful performers -- means being condemned to a life without school, education, or clean water, marriage and babies coming too early, too many births, children who die of preventable diseases, backbreaking work in the fields, subordination to husband and his family, and an early death.

Every year, almost 12 million children under the age of five needlessly die of infectious diseases associated with poverty. But each additional year spent by their mothers in primary school lowers the risk of premature child deaths by about 8 percent. In Pakistan, an extra year of school for 1,000 girls could prevent sixty infant deaths.

With women and girls being the main farmers in Africa and southern Asia, their education offers a chance to develop more efficient farming practices, improve output, and raise awareness of the ecological needs of the land with tree planting and farming. Therefore, the world community cannot afford to ignore this avenue of change.

What does the author think of girls' education?

A
essential
✓ Correct
B
terrible
C
indifferent
D
helpless
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

The passage calls educating girls “a real lifesaver” and says it is the surest way to reduce poverty, raising productivity and lowering death and birth rates. Thus the author views girls' education as essential. “Terrible,” “indifferent,” and “helpless” contradict the strong positive claim that girls must be educated rather than kept from school.

Winner of 5 Olympic Gold Medals

📖 Shared Reading Comprehension Passage (high9427) Shared ID: PSG_DDD9F1C6ACD7 · Question 2 of 2

No matter where he lives, 16-year-old Danny Lopez feels like an outsider: he is half-Mexican and half-white.

At his private high school in wealthy northern San Diego, California, US, Lopez is too brown to fit in, whereas for the Mexican side of his family in National City, just a dozen miles from the border, he is too white to belong.

Different from both sides, Lopez is silent in school. He focuses on his passion for baseball and working hard to improve the pitches that have kept him off the school team.

Mexican Whiteboy, by Matt de la Pena, is about a teenager's search for identity. It was named as one of the Top Ten Best Books for Young Adults in 2009 by the US Young Adult Library Services Association.

When Lopez's mother decides to go to live with her wealthy white boyfriend in San Francisco, he chooses to spend the summer with his father's family in San Diego. It's a trip to explore roots and self-identity, filled with unexpected friendship.

There he meets Uno, of mixed heritage like himself, also with a divorced mom. Uno understands Danny's split background and helps him improve his baseball skills. Both boys have big league dreams, but they both have to learn to come to terms with their mixed heritages before they can achieve their goals.

Aside from discussions of racism, Mexican Whiteboy takes on other issues, such as the importance of family and the negative influence of hiding the truth. It also shows how sports can draw cultures together.

When Lopez found it is hard for him to fit in, he _ .

A
starts writing a book about himself
B
begins to look for identity with the help of Mexican Whiteboy
C
loses his interest in baseball
D
works in the fields in which he was kept off the school team
✓ Correct
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option D)

The passage states that Lopez, unable to fit into either community, remains silent at school and focuses on baseball, working hard to improve the pitches that kept him off the school team. Thus D is supported. A and B introduce unsupported actions; C contradicts the text because he does not lose interest in baseball.

Ó bófin mu láti máa dá àwọn ẹranko lóró tàbí láti máa kó wọn sí wàhálà nígbà tí wọ́n bá ń ṣe ìwádìí, àmọ́ kìkì tí wọ́n bá dín in kù, tí wọ́n sì pa wọ́n ní kíákíá bó bá ti lè ṣeé ṣe tó.

📖 Shared Reading Comprehension Passage (high9427) Shared ID: PSG_DDD9F1C6ACD7 · Question 1 of 2

No matter where he lives, 16-year-old Danny Lopez feels like an outsider: he is half-Mexican and half-white.

At his private high school in wealthy northern San Diego, California, US, Lopez is too brown to fit in, whereas for the Mexican side of his family in National City, just a dozen miles from the border, he is too white to belong.

Different from both sides, Lopez is silent in school. He focuses on his passion for baseball and working hard to improve the pitches that have kept him off the school team.

Mexican Whiteboy, by Matt de la Pena, is about a teenager's search for identity. It was named as one of the Top Ten Best Books for Young Adults in 2009 by the US Young Adult Library Services Association.

When Lopez's mother decides to go to live with her wealthy white boyfriend in San Francisco, he chooses to spend the summer with his father's family in San Diego. It's a trip to explore roots and self-identity, filled with unexpected friendship.

There he meets Uno, of mixed heritage like himself, also with a divorced mom. Uno understands Danny's split background and helps him improve his baseball skills. Both boys have big league dreams, but they both have to learn to come to terms with their mixed heritages before they can achieve their goals.

Aside from discussions of racism, Mexican Whiteboy takes on other issues, such as the importance of family and the negative influence of hiding the truth. It also shows how sports can draw cultures together.

Most probably "Mexican Whiteboy" is a _ .

A
book
✓ Correct
B
club
C
newspaper
D
organization
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

The passage identifies "Mexican Whiteboy" as a work by Matt de la Pena and notes it was named among the Top Ten Best Books for Young Adults. Therefore, it is most probably a book. Option B, club, is unsupported; C, newspaper, and D, organization, are not mentioned or implied by the bibliographic clues.

#248557 · SUBLANGENG_Q496331 DB2_PYQ 📖 Passage PSG_B4750263CA2F (Question 2 of 2) UNIVERSAL: ALL_COUNTRIES SUB_LANG_ENGLISH — English Language, Reading Comprehension & Verbal Ability Hard PYQ: National Standardized English Reading Comprehension Examination (2018-2023)Body: Official Standardized Examination Board · ALL_COUNTRIES

📖 Shared Reading Comprehension Passage (high9774) Shared ID: PSG_B4750263CA2F · Question 2 of 2

STRANGERS parties go wild these days. The main idea of them is to never let the people know who their matches are until the last minute. These are some popular practices that are storming the world's youth on this V-day.

Speed dating

It's exactly what the name implies. Complete with a timer, a whistle and 50 willing singles, speed dating is not the traditional dinner and a movie type of first date. Singles pay US USD 35 for three minutes with more than two dozen future dates. They ask questions and try to get to know each other. At the sound of a whistle, they do the same thing all over again--25 times in all.

Lock and key party

Women are given a lock and men are given a key, upon arriving at the party. Each key and lock has several matches. One can hunt for his/her later-to-be sweetheart in various groups, and try out the key or lock. Each time a lock and key match the pair are entered into a ballot for top prizes!

Dinner in the dark

Waiters wearing night vision glasses guide the guests through the pitch-black dining room. They are seated at the table and familiarized with the wine glass and plate. Food is handled with the fingers. People will not see the food they are eating or the guests at their table until the dessert course. At that moment, dining partners will appear.

Message party

Have A Cocktail, Leave A Message! When you arrive, you get a tag with a number and a pad of post-it notes. If you see someone you like, write a message on the message board. But wait... You've got a message... Go and collect it! You can make new friends and win lots of gifts. The more messages you leave on the bulletin board, the more chances you have of winning prizes.

In which party does a participant have to be careful with the time?

A
Speed dating.
✓ Correct
B
Lock and key party.
C
Dinner in the dark.
D
Message party.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

Speed dating uses a timer and whistle, with singles spending three minutes per date and repeating the process many times. Therefore, that is the party where participants must watch time carefully. Lock and key party, dinner in the dark, and message party are not described with a timer in the excerpt.

A
All the parties have very strict rules for their members.
B
If you want to go to any of the parties, you have to buy a ticket.
C
All the parties are aimed at strangers who want to be lovers.
✓ Correct
D
All the parties are suitable not only for the young but for the old.

Èwo nínú àwọn ìṣẹ̀lẹ̀ wọ̀nyí ni ó ṣeé ṣe kí ìwà olùjẹ́jọ́ mú un jẹ́ ẹni tó jẹ̀bi ẹ̀sùn ọ̀daràn fún ikú olùfaragbà.

A
Olùjẹ́jọ́ kan yín ìbọn fún olùfaragbà lórí. Wọ́n gbé olùfaragbà náà lọ sí ilé-ìwòsàn fún ìtọ́jú ọgbẹ́ náà. Lẹ́yìn náà Ilẹ̀ ríri kan kọlu ìlú náà, tí ó jẹ́ kí ilé ìwòsàn náà wó lulẹ̀. Olùfaragbà náà ni àwókù ilé náà wó pa.
B
Olùjẹ́jọ́ kan àti olùfaragbà kan jọ ń gbé nínú ilé kan. Olùjẹ́jọ́ náà mọ̀ wípé olùfaragbà náà ń ní àjọṣepọ̀ ìfẹ́ pẹ̀lú obìnrin tó ti gbéyàwó. Ní ọjọ́ kan, olùjẹ́jọ́ náà gbọ́ wípé ọkọ obìnrin yìí fẹ́ pa olùfaragbà náà. Bí ó tilẹ̀ jẹ́ wípé olùjẹ́jọ́ náà ní àkókò tó pọ̀ láti kìlọ̀ fún olùfaragbà náà, ó kọ̀ láti ṣe bẹ́ẹ̀. Ní alẹ́ ọjọ́ náà, ọkọ arábìnrin yìí fi ọ̀bẹ gùn olùfaragba náà pa.
C
Olùfaragbà kan, tí ó jẹ́ atatẹ́tẹ́, jẹ́ àwọn jàǹdùkú ní gbèsè tó pọ̀ gan-an. Olùjẹ́jọ́ kan, tó jẹ́ ògbóǹkangí àwọn jàǹdùkú, ń halẹ̀ mọ́ ọkùnrin náà pé òun yóò pá tí kò bá san owó tó jẹ́. Pẹ̀lú ìbẹ̀rù, olùfaragbà náà wọ ọkọ̀ òfuurufú to kan kúrò ní ìlú. Ọkọ̀ òfuurufú náà já, ó sì pa olùfaragbà náà.
D
Olùjẹ́jọ́ kan àti olùfaragbà kan jọ ń wa ọkọ̀ papọ̀ lọ sí ibi iṣẹ́ nígbà tí olùjẹ́jọ́ náà, fi ọbẹ̀ gún un ní apá láì ṣe ohunkóhun láti bíi nínú. Olùjẹ́jọ́ náà tì í jáde kúrò nínú ọkọ̀ ayọ́kẹ́lẹ́ náà. Olùfaragbà náà ṣubú sí ẹ̀gbẹ́ ọ̀nà ó sì fọ́ ní kókósẹ̀. Nítorí pé kò lè dìde kúrò láàárín ọ̀nà, ọkọ̀ ayọ́kẹ́lẹ́ kan tí awakọ̀ tó ti mutí yó kan ń wà tẹ olùfaragbà náà pa ní ǹkan bíi ọgbọ̀n-ìṣẹ́jú lẹ́yìn náà.
✓ Correct
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option D)

Correct Answer: (D) Olùjẹ́jọ́ kan àti olùfaragbà kan jọ ń wa ọkọ̀ papọ̀ lọ sí ibi iṣẹ́ nígbà tí olùjẹ́jọ́ náà, fi ọbẹ̀ gún un ní apá láì ṣe ohunkóhun láti bíi nínú. Olùjẹ́jọ́ náà tì í jáde kúrò nínú ọkọ̀ ayọ́kẹ́lẹ́ náà. Olùfaragbà náà ṣubú sí ẹ̀gbẹ́ ọ̀nà ó sì fọ́ ní kókósẹ̀. Nítorí pé kò lè dìde kúrò láàárín ọ̀nà, ọkọ̀ ayọ́kẹ́lẹ́ kan tí awakọ̀ tó ti mutí yó kan ń wà tẹ olùfaragbà náà pa ní ǹkan bíi ọgbọ̀n-ìṣẹ́jú lẹ́yìn náà. [Subject: Professional Law · Medium: EN].

Ọkùnrin kan wá gba ayàwòrán fídíò síṣẹ́ láti ya fídíò ìgbéyàwó ọmọbìnrin rẹ̀. Ìwé àdéhùn tí wọ́n kọ sínú ìwé náà ní "ọ̀rọ̀ kan nínú tó sọ pé wọ́n máa "san 10,000 fún fídíò ìgbéyàwó náà àti àpèjẹ náà fún odindi ìṣẹ́jú 60.” Ọkùnrin náà fi kún ìwé àdéhùn náà pé àwọn ohun èlò tó ní ìtumọ̀ gíga ló máa fi ṣe fídíò náà. Àdéhùn náà kò mẹ́nu kan àwọn nǹkan mìíràn tó ní í ṣe pẹ̀lú owó ìtaràn. Lẹ́yìn náà, ayàwòrán náà ya fídíò náà tó gùn tó ìṣẹ́jú 60, ó sì tún un ṣe, ó lo àwọn ohun èlò tó ń mú kí fídíò náà ṣe kedere, ó sì fi hàn án fún ọkùnrin náà. Lẹ́yìn náà, ayàwòrán fídíò náà fún ọkùnrin náà ní ẹ̀rí owó tó jẹ́ 15,000. Yàtọ̀ sí iye tí wọ́n san fún iṣẹ́ náà, ìyẹn 10,000, owó tí wọ́n fi gba àwọn ẹ̀rọ gíga fíìmù náà jẹ́ 5,000. Èwo nínú àwọn àríyànjiyàn tó wà nísàlẹ̀ yìí ló ṣeé ṣe jù lọ láti tì ayàwòrán lẹ́yìn nínú àlàyé rẹ̀ pé nígbà tí ìwé àdéhùn tí wọ́n kọ sílẹ̀ náà bá wáyé, ọkùnrin náà gbà láti san 5,000 fún ayàwòrán fídíò fún lílo àwọn ohun èlò gíga-ìtumọ̀ láfikún sí owó 10,000?

A
Níbàámu pẹ̀lú àṣà ìṣòwò tó wọ́pọ̀ lágbo iṣẹ́ fídíò, owó tí wọ́n máa ń san fún fídíò yíyà tó 10,000 túmọ̀ sí pé wọ́n á san 10,000 lẹ́yìn owó àfikún tí wọ́n máa ń san bí wọ́n bá lo àwọn ẹ̀rọ tó ń gbé àwòrán jáde lọ́nà tó ṣe kedere.
✓ Correct
B
Àdéhùn tí wọ́n bá ṣe ní ẹnu, tí wọ́n bá lè fi hàn pé ó wà níbàámu, yóò wulẹ̀ jẹ́ àfikún, kì í ṣe àtakò sí "àlàkalẹ̀ ìsanwó" tí wọ́n kọ.
C
Lábẹ́ àwọn òfin tó ń ṣàkóso káràkátá ọjà ní orílẹ̀-èdè Amẹ́ríkà, tí àwọn ẹ̀rí tí kò fojú hàn bá wà, àwọn àlàyé míràn tí ẹgbẹ́ méjèèjì jọ fara mọ́ jẹ́ ìtẹ́wọ́gbà àyàfi tí àwọn àlàyé náà “bá yàtọ̀ tàbí lòdì sí àwọn èyí tó wà nínú àdéhùn àkọsílẹ̀ náà.”
D
Nítorí àríyànjiyàn wípé “àkọsílẹ̀ nípa bí a ṣe ń sanwó” jẹ́ síso papọ̀ tí kò sì hàn gbangba pé kò ṣe kedere tàbí ohun tí ó ṣe kedere, ohun tí ó yẹ kí wọ́n ṣe pèsè fún lílo àwọn ẹ̀rí tí kò fojú hàn tí wọ́n bá wà fún àǹfààní àwọn ẹ̀gbẹ́ méjèèjì tí ọ̀rọ̀ náà kan, nígbà tí ó jẹ́ wípé olùyafídíò náà yóò pàdánù ẹgbẹ̀rún márùn-ún dọ́là (USD 5,000) lórí àdéhùn àkọsílẹ̀ náà.
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

Correct Answer: (A) Níbàámu pẹ̀lú àṣà ìṣòwò tó wọ́pọ̀ lágbo iṣẹ́ fídíò, owó tí wọ́n máa ń san fún fídíò yíyà tó 10,000 túmọ̀ sí pé wọ́n á san 10,000 lẹ́yìn owó àfikún tí wọ́n máa ń san bí wọ́n bá lo àwọn ẹ̀rọ tó ń gbé àwòrán jáde lọ́nà tó ṣe kedere. [Subject: Professional Law · Medium: EN].

📖 Shared Reading Comprehension Passage (high5825) Shared ID: PSG_CC6D7A783749 · Question 1 of 1

Poet William Stafford once said that we are defined more by the detours in life than by the narrow road toward goals.I like this image.But it was quite by accident that I discovered the deep meaning of his words.

For years we made the long drive from our home in Seattle to my parents' home in Boise in nine hours.We traveled the way most people do:the fastest,shortest,easiest road,especially when I was alone with four noisy,restless kids who hate confinement and have strong opinions about everything.

Road trips felt risky,so I would drive fast,stopping only when I had to.We would stick to the freeways and arrive tired.

But then Banner,our lamb was born.He was rejected by his mama days before our planned trip to Boise.I had two choices:leave Banner with my husband,or take him with me.My husband made the decision for me.

That is how I found myself on the road with four kids,a baby lamb and nothing but my everlasting optimism to see me through.We took the country roads out of necessity.We had to stop every hour,let Banner shake out his legs and feed him.The kids chased him and one another.They'd get back in the car breathless and energized,smelling fresh from the cold air.

We explored side roads,catching grasshoppers in waisthigh grass.Even if we simply looked out of the car windows at baby pigs following their mother,or fish leaping out of the water,it was better than the best ride down the freeway.Here was life.And new horizons .

We eventually arrived at my parents' doorstep astonishingly fresh and full of stories.

I grew brave with the trip back home and creative with my disciplining technique.On an empty section of road,everyone started quarreling.I stopped the car,ordered all kids out and told them to meet me up ahead.I parked my car half a mile away and read my book in sweet silence.

Some road trips are by necessity fast and straight.But that trip with Banner opened our eyes to a world available to anyone adventurous enough to wander around and made me realize that a detour may uncover the best part of a journey-and the best part of yourself.

What could be the best title for the passage?

A
Charm of the Detour
✓ Correct
B
The Road to Bravery
C
Creativity out of Necessity
D
Road Trip and Country Life
View Explanation & Provenance (Answer: Option A)

The passage opens with Stafford’s idea that detours define us, then contrasts the usual fast freeway trip with an accidental, slower experience involving Banner the lamb. Therefore, the best title is Charm of the Detour. The Road to Bravery lacks evidence of bravery; Creativity out of Necessity is unsupported; Road Trip and Country Life is too broad and misses the detour theme.

✓ Correct
